### [1] [Causal AI For AMS Circuit Design: Interpretable Parameter Effects Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24618)
*Mohyeu Hussain,David Koblah,Reiner Dizon-Paradis,Domenic Forte*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果推断的AMS电路建模框架，通过发现DAG和估计平均处理效应来量化参数影响，在三种运算放大器上的测试显示其预测误差低于25%，显著优于偏差超过80%的神经网络方法，兼具高准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟混合信号（AMS）电路具有高度非线性特性且处理连续的现实世界信号，这使得使用数据驱动的AI方法进行建模比数字模块困难得多。现有方法难以有效连接结构化设计数据（器件尺寸、偏置电压等）与实际性能表现。

Method: 提出了一种因果推断框架，首先从SPICE仿真数据中发现有向无环图（DAG），然后通过平均处理效应（ATE）估计来量化参数影响。该方法基于TSMC 65nm工艺实现的三种运算放大器系列（OTA、望远镜式和折叠共源共栅）进行评估，并与基准神经网络回归器进行对比。

Result: 在所有测试电路中，因果模型能够以小于25%的平均绝对误差重现基于仿真的ATE值，而神经网络方法的偏差超过80%且经常预测错误的符号方向。这表明因果AI方法在准确性和可解释性方面都显著优于传统神经网络方法。

Conclusion: 因果AI方法为AMS电路设计提供了更高的准确性和可解释性，能够产生人类可理解的设计旋钮排序和明确的"假设"预测，使设计者能够理解尺寸和拓扑结构之间的权衡关系，为更高效、可信赖的AMS设计自动化铺平了道路。

Abstract: Analog-mixed-signal (AMS) circuits are highly non-linear and operate on continuous real-world signals, making them far more difficult to model with data-driven AI than digital blocks. To close the gap between structured design data (device dimensions, bias voltages, etc.) and real-world performance, we propose a causal-inference framework that first discovers a directed-acyclic graph (DAG) from SPICE simulation data and then quantifies parameter impact through Average Treatment Effect (ATE) estimation. The approach yields human-interpretable rankings of design knobs and explicit 'what-if' predictions, enabling designers to understand trade-offs in sizing and topology. We evaluate the pipeline on three operational-amplifier families (OTA, telescopic, and folded-cascode) implemented in TSMC 65nm and benchmark it against a baseline neural-network (NN) regressor. Across all circuits the causal model reproduces simulation-based ATEs with an average absolute error of less than 25%, whereas the neural network deviates by more than 80% and frequently predicts the wrong sign. These results demonstrate that causal AI provides both higher accuracy and explainability, paving the way for more efficient, trustworthy AMS design automation.

</details>


### [2] [TAMI-MPC:Trusted Acceleration of Minimal-Interaction MPC for Efficient Nonlinear Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24861)
*Zhuoran Li,Hanieh Totonchi Asl,Yifei Cai,Ebrahim Nouri,Danella Zhao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TAMI-MPC框架，通过可信执行环境（TEE）辅助最小化交互的多方安全计算，显著降低了非线性推理中的通信开销和计算负担，并在ResNet-50和BERT-base推理上分别实现了最高4.86倍和7.44倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多方安全计算（MPC）系统在隐私保护机器学习推理中，严重依赖通信和计算密集型原语（如非线性推理中的安全比较），这在资源受限平台上往往难以实现实时推理。 oblivious transfer (OT)是已知的计算瓶颈，现有工作对此依赖过重。

Method: 引入了TAMI-MPC框架，通过重新设计核心原语（叶节点比较和树合并），将每次操作的交互轮数从log(n)降至1。利用TEE内的同步种子消除了大部分设计对OT的依赖，并结合关联随机数重用技术保持计算轻量。设计了专用加速器，重构各阶段数据流以实现连续、细粒度流处理和高并行度，从而降低内存开销。

Result: 在ResNet-50推理中，相比最先进的CNN框架实现了最高4.86倍加速；在BERT-base推理中，相比最先进的LLM框架实现了最高7.44倍加速。

Conclusion: TAMI-MPC框架成功解决了资源受限平台上MPC非线性推理的效率和实用性问题，为边缘设备上的隐私保护机器学习提供了可行的实时推理解决方案。

Abstract: Secure multi-party computation (MPC) offers a practical foundation for privacy-preserving machine learning at the edge. However, current MPC systems rely heavily on communication and computation-intensive primitives-such as secure comparison for nonlinear inference, which are often impractical on resource-constrained platforms. To enable real-time inference under a resource-constrained platform, we introduce a Trusted Acceleration of Minimal-Interaction MPC framework, TAMI-MPC, for nonlinear evaluation. Specifically, we reduce communication cost by redesigning the core primitives, leaf comparison, and tree merge, reducing the interactive round from log(n) to just 1 per operation. Furthermore, unlike prior work that heavily relies on oblivious transfer (OT), a well-known computational bottleneck, we leverage synchronized seeds inside the TEE to eliminate OT for the vast majority of our designs, along with a correlated-randomness reuse technique that keeps new designs computationally lightweight. To fully realize the potential, we design a specialized accelerator that restructures the dataflow across stages to enable continuous, fine-grained streaming and high parallelism, reducing memory overhead. Our design achieves up to 4.86x speedup on ResNet-50 inference, compared with state-of-the-art CNN frameworks, and achieves up to 7.44x speedup on BERT-base inference, compared with state-of-the-art LLM frameworks.

</details>


### [3] [Surrogates, Spikes, and Sparsity: Performance Analysis and Characterization of SNN Hyperparameters on Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24891)
*Ilkin Aliyev,Jesus Lopez,Tosiron Adegbija*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对SNN训练超参数与硬件推理效率脱节的问题，通过系统化的工作负载特征分析，揭示了替代梯度函数和神经元模型选择对硬件延迟的关键影响，并在FPGA平台上验证了稀疏感知的超参数选择可显著提升准确率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）虽然理论上具备通过稀疏、事件驱动计算实现低功耗推理的优势，但训练时的选择与推理时的稀疏性之间存在不透明的关系，导致理论能效优势往往无法在实际硬件性能中体现。现有研究多关注权重剪枝和压缩，而训练超参数（特别是替代梯度函数和神经元模型配置）对硬件级激活稀疏性的影响尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出了一种量化SNN超参数对硬件延迟敏感性的工作负载特征分析方法。研究解耦了替代梯度函数（如Fast Sigmoid、Spike Rate Escape）和神经元模型（LIF、Lapicque）对分类准确率和推理效率的影响，并在三个事件视觉数据集（DVS128-Gesture、N-MNIST、DVS-CIFAR10）上进行实验。通过构建自定义的周期精确FPGA SNN仪器平台进行验证。

Result: 研究发现标准准确率指标是硬件效率的不良预测因子。虽然Fast Sigmoid在DVS-CIFAR10上实现了最高准确率，但Spike Rate Escape在DVS128-Gesture上将推理延迟降低了12.2%，且准确率损失极小。神经元模型选择与参数调优同等重要：从LIF切换到Lapicque神经元可实现高达28%的延迟降低。在FPGA平台验证中，稀疏感知的超参数选择相比基线可将准确率提升9.1%，延迟降低超过2倍。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一套从训练参数预测硬件行为的方法论，证明了训练时的超参数选择对SNN硬件推理效率具有决定性影响。研究成果为SNN硬件加速器的设计优化提供了重要指导，相关RTL代码和复现工件已开源。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer inherent advantages for low-power inference through sparse, event-driven computation. However, the theoretical energy benefits of SNNs are often decoupled from real hardware performance due to the opaque relationship between training-time choices and inference-time sparsity. While prior work has focused on weight pruning and compression, the role of training hyperparameters -- specifically surrogate gradient functions and neuron model configurations -- in shaping hardware-level activation sparsity remains underexplored.
  This paper presents a workload characterization study quantifying the sensitivity of hardware latency to SNN hyperparameters. We decouple the impact of surrogate gradient functions (e.g., Fast Sigmoid, Spike Rate Escape) and neuron models (LIF, Lapicque) on classification accuracy and inference efficiency across three event-based vision datasets: DVS128-Gesture, N-MNIST, and DVS-CIFAR10. Our analysis reveals that standard accuracy metrics are poor predictors of hardware efficiency. While Fast Sigmoid achieves the highest accuracy on DVS-CIFAR10, Spike Rate Escape reduces inference latency by up to 12.2% on DVS128-Gesture with minimal accuracy trade-offs. We also demonstrate that neuron model selection is as critical as parameter tuning; transitioning from LIF to Lapicque neurons yields up to 28% latency reduction. We validate on a custom cycle-accurate FPGA-based SNN instrumentation platform, showing that sparsity-aware hyperparameter selection can improve accuracy by 9.1% and latency by over 2x compared to baselines. These findings establish a methodology for predicting hardware behavior from training parameters. The RTL and reproducibility artifacts are at https://zenodo.org/records/18893738.

</details>


### [4] [AutoPDR: Circuit-Aware Solver Configuration Prediction for Hardware Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25048)
*Guangyu Hu,Chen Chen,Xiaofeng Zhou,Jiaxi Zhang,Wei Zhang,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于图学习的电路感知求解器配置框架，用于PDR形式化验证的自动化参数调优，通过结合图表示、静态特征和专家知识约束，大幅减少了搜索空间并显著提升了验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDR算法在硬件和软件系统形式化验证中表现出色，但其性能对参数配置高度敏感。手动参数调优耗时且需要领域专业知识，而传统的自动化参数调优框架并不适合PDR这类时间敏感的验证任务。

Method: 提出电路感知求解器配置框架，结合图表示与静态电路特征预测最优PDR求解配置；引入基于约束的参数过滤机制融入专家先验知识，消除无效和低效配置；设计特征提取流水线捕获电路拓扑和组件模式的结构、功能和连接特性。

Result: 约束过滤减少78%搜索空间；在综合基准测试套件上显著优于默认配置和常用设置；系统成功识别特定电路的参数模式并自动选择最适合的求解策略。

Conclusion: 该框架能够根据电路特性自动选择最优求解策略，显著提升PDR验证性能，是自动化形式化验证工作流中的实用工具。

Abstract: Property Directed Reachability (PDR) is a powerful algorithm for formal verification of hardware and software systems, but its performance is highly sensitive to parameter configurations. Manual parameter tuning is time-consuming and requires domain expertise, while traditional automated parameter tuning frameworks are not well-suited for time-sensitive verification tasks like PDR. This paper presents a circuit-aware solver configuration framework that employs graph learning for intelligent heuristic selection in PDR-based verification. Our approach combines graph representations with static circuit features to predict optimal PDR solving configurations for specific circuits. We incorporate expert prior knowledge through constraint-based parameter filtering to eliminate invalid and inefficient configurations and reduce 78% search space. Our feature extraction pipeline captures structural, functional, and connectivity characteristics of circuit topology and component patterns. Experimental evaluation on a comprehensive benchmark suite demonstrates significant performance improvements compared to default configurations and commonly-used settings. The system successfully identifies circuit-specific parameter patterns and automatically selects the most suitable solving strategies based on circuit characteristics, making it a practical tool for automated formal verification workflows.

</details>


### [5] [FluxEDA: A Unified Execution Infrastructure for Stateful Agentic EDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25243)
*Zhengrui Chen,Zixuan Song,Yu Li,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了FluxEDA，一个统一的、有状态的EDA基础设施底座，通过托管网关接口和持久化后端实例解决了现有EDA自动化集成中难以保持工具状态和支持迭代优化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型与EDA工具集成主要依赖脚本级或请求级交互，难以在生产环境中保持工具状态并支持迭代优化。

Method: 设计了FluxEDA框架，引入基于网关的托管执行接口处理结构化请求与响应，并维护持久化后端实例，使上层代理和客户端可通过保留的运行时状态与异构EDA工具交互。

Result: 在两个商业后端案例（自动后布线时序ECO和标准单元子库优化）上的评估表明，FluxEDA能够支持真实工具环境下的多步分析与优化，包括状态复用、回滚和协调迭代执行。

Conclusion: 有状态且受管理的基础设施层是构建代理辅助EDA自动化的实用基础。

Abstract: Large language models and autonomous agents are increasingly explored for EDA automation, but many existing integrations still rely on script-level or request-level interactions, which makes it difficult to preserve tool state and support iterative optimization in real production-oriented environments. In this work, we present FluxEDA, a unified and stateful infrastructure substrate for agentic EDA. FluxEDA introduces a managed gateway-based execution interface with structured request and response handling. It also maintains persistent backend instances. Together, these features allow upper-layer agents and programmable clients to interact with heterogeneous EDA tools through preserved runtime state, rather than through isolated shell invocations. We evaluate the framework using two representative commercial backend case studies: automated post-route timing ECO and standard-cell sub-library optimization. The results show that FluxEDA can support multi-step analysis and optimization over real tool contexts, including state reuse, rollback, and coordinated iterative execution. These findings suggest that a stateful and governed infrastructure layer is a practical foundation for agent-assisted EDA automation.

</details>

### [6] [Experimental Analysis of FreeRTOS Dependability through Targeted Fault Injection Campaigns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25666)
*Luca Mannella,Stefano Di Carlo,Alessandro Savino*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了KRONOS框架，用于评估FreeRTOS在电离辐射环境下的可靠性，发现指针和调度器相关变量的损坏常导致系统崩溃，而许多TCB字段对系统可用性的影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时操作系统（RTOS）在安全关键领域具有重要作用，需要确定性和可预测的任务执行。然而，它们越来越多地暴露于电离辐射环境中，这可能损害系统的可靠性。因此需要评估FreeRTOS在此类条件下的表现。

Method: 引入了KRONOS，这是一个基于软件的非侵入式后传播故障注入（FI）框架，能够在无需专用硬件或调试接口的情况下，向操作系统可见的内核数据结构注入瞬态和永久性故障。使用该框架对FreeRTOS内核核心组件（包括调度器相关变量和任务控制块TCB）进行了广泛的故障注入实验，分析了内核级损坏对功能正确性、时序行为和可用性的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，指针和关键调度器相关变量的损坏频繁导致系统崩溃，而许多TCB字段的损坏对系统可用性的影响有限。

Conclusion: KRONOS框架为评估RTOS在辐射环境下的可靠性提供了有效手段，研究结果揭示了不同内核数据结构损坏对系统行为影响的差异，为RTOS的容错设计和可靠性优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Real-Time Operating Systems (RTOSes) play a crucial role in safety-critical domains, where deterministic and predictable task execution is essential. Yet they are increasingly exposed to ionizing radiation, which can compromise system dependability.
  To assess FreeRTOS under such conditions, we introduce KRONOS, a software-based, non-intrusive post-propagation Fault Injection (FI) framework that injects transient and permanent faults into Operating System-visible kernel data structures without specialized hardware or debug interfaces. Using KRONOS, we conduct an extensive FI campaign on core FreeRTOS kernel components, including scheduler-related variables and Task Control Blocks (TCBs), characterizing the impact of kernel-level corruptions on functional correctness, timing behavior, and availability.
  The results show that corruption of pointer and key scheduler-related variables frequently leads to crashes, whereas many TCB fields have only a limited impact on system availability.

</details>

### [7] [Decentralized Task Scheduling in Distributed Systems: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24738)
*Daniel Benniah John*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于NumPy轻量级实现的去中心化多智能体深度强化学习（DRL-MADRL）任务调度框架，在异构分布式系统中实现了任务完成时间缩短15.6%、能效提升15.2%以及SLA满意度提升至82.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式系统中的任务调度面临动态工作负载、异构资源和竞争性服务质量要求的挑战。传统的中心化方法存在可扩展性限制和单点故障问题，而经典启发式算法缺乏对变化条件的适应性。

Method: 将问题建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP），开发了一种仅使用NumPy实现的轻量级Actor-Critic架构，使得框架能够在资源受限的边缘设备上部署，无需依赖重量级机器学习框架。

Result: 在100节点异构系统上处理每轮1000个任务的实验表明：平均任务完成时间缩短15.6%（30.8秒 vs 36.5秒随机基线），能效提升15.2%（745.2 kWh vs 878.3 kWh），SLA满意度达82.3%（基线为75.5%），所有改进均具有统计显著性（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了去中心化多智能体深度强化学习方法在异构分布式系统任务调度中的有效性，其轻量级实现特性使其适合部署在资源受限的边缘设备上，为分布式系统调度提供了可扩展、高能效且满足QoS要求的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient task scheduling in large-scale distributed systems presents significant challenges due to dynamic workloads, heterogeneous resources, and competing quality-of-service requirements. Traditional centralized approaches face scalability limitations and single points of failure, while classical heuristics lack adaptability to changing conditions. This paper proposes a decentralized multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (DRL-MADRL) framework for task scheduling in heterogeneous distributed systems. We formulate the problem as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) and develop a lightweight actor-critic architecture implemented using only NumPy, enabling deployment on resource-constrained edge devices without heavyweight machine learning frameworks. Using workload characteristics derived from the publicly available Google Cluster Trace dataset, we evaluate our approach on a 100-node heterogeneous system processing 1,000 tasks per episode over 30 experimental runs. Experimental results demonstrate 15.6% improvement in average task completion time (30.8s vs 36.5s for random baseline), 15.2% energy efficiency gain (745.2 kWh vs 878.3 kWh), and 82.3% SLA satisfaction compared to 75.5% for baselines, with all improvements statistically significant (p < 0.001). The lightweight implementation requires only NumPy, Matplotlib, and SciPy. Complete source code and experimental data are provided for full reproducibility at https://github.com/danielbenniah/marl-distributed-scheduling.

</details>


### [8] [Rafture: Erasure-coded Raft with Post-Dissemination Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24761)
*Rithwik Kerur,Divyakant Agrawal,Michael K. Reiter,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Rafture，一种新颖的信息分散算法及其在共识协议中的集成。它是首个引入传播后剪枝机制的解决方案，允许系统在传播完成后自适应调整存储成本。通过使用简单的固定阈值纠删码并改变片段分配，Rafture确保重建始终可以从F+1个片段完成且无需额外元数据，同时节点能够基于本地观察信息自主适应，无需全局协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式系统中有效传播和存储纠删码数据面临挑战，特别是当信息分散与共识协议集成时，需要应对不可预测的网络延迟。现有方法存在三个主要局限性：几乎只关注传播时效率；恢复过程复杂且需要可能因共识值而异的元数据；依赖中央领导者为所有节点做出存储决策。

Method: Rafture采用简单固定阈值纠删码，通过沿第二维度改变不同片段分配来确保重建始终可用F+1个片段完成且无需额外元数据。引入传播后剪枝机制，允许系统在传播完成后调整存储成本。节点基于本地观察信息自主做出存储决策，消除了全局协调需求。

Result: 在高度动态网络环境下的评估表明，Rafture简化了恢复过程，同时在可变网络条件下显著改善了长期存储消耗。

Conclusion: Rafture成功克服了现有信息分散解决方案的局限性，通过引入传播后剪枝机制和自主节点适应能力，实现了更高效和灵活的分布式存储，同时简化了恢复过程并降低了存储开销。

Abstract: Spreading and storing erasure-coded data in distributed systems effectively is challenging in real settings. Practical deployments must contend with unpredictable network latencies, particularly when information dispersal is integrated into consensus protocols, a prominent and latency-sensitive use case. Existing approaches address this challenge through timeout-based dissemination and adaptive communication or storage decisions driven by acknowledgments during dissemination. However, these designs focus almost exclusively on dissemination-time efficiency, complicate recovery with reconstruction procedures that require metadata that can differ per consensus value, and rely on a centralized leader to make storage decisions for all nodes.
  This paper introduces \textbf{Rafture}, a novel information dispersal algorithm, and its integration in a consensus protocol, that overcomes these limitations. Rafture is the first information dispersal solution to incorporate post-dissemination pruning, allowing systems to adapt storage costs after dissemination completes. It employs a simple, fixed-threshold erasure code while varying distinct fragment assignment along a second dimension. This ensures that reconstruction is always possible from $F+1$ fragments using the same interpolation method and no additional metadata. Rafture further enables nodes to adapt autonomously based on locally observed information, eliminating the need for global coordination.
  We evaluate Rafture in highly dynamic network settings and show that it simplifies recovery while significantly improving long-term storage consumption under variable network conditions.

</details>


### [9] [eBeeMetrics: An eBPF-based Library Framework for Feedback-free Observability of QoS Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25067)
*Muntaka Ibnath,Mohammadreza Rezvani,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了eBeeMetrics，一个基于eBPF的框架，通过观测系统调用等内核事件来准确推断应用级的QoS指标（如尾延迟和吞吐量），从而简化系统管理运行时与QoS反馈的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的系统管理运行时（如资源管理和功耗管理）依赖QoS指标作为反馈，但这些指标通常无法通过硬件性能计数器观测，也无法在操作系统内核中直接观测，导致应用检测复杂且开销大。

Method: 设计了eBeeMetrics，这是一个基于eBPF的库框架，仅利用eBPF可观测的事件（如系统调用）来推导应用级指标。它可以作为即插即用的替代方案，将系统管理运行时与QoS指标反馈解耦，或作为现有指标的补充。

Result: 实验结果表明，eBeeMetrics在各种延迟敏感型工作负载中，与实际测量的吞吐量和延迟指标实现了强相关性，证明了其推断的准确性。

Conclusion: eBeeMetrics成功填补了内核级可观测事件与应用级QoS指标之间的空白，为系统管理运行时提供了一种低开销、易集成的观测方案，且已开源。

Abstract: Many system management runtimes (SMRs), such as resource management and power management techniques, rely on quality-of-service (QoS) metrics, such as tail latency or throughput, as feedback. These QoS metrics are generally neither observable with hardware performance counters nor directly observable within the OS kernel. This introduces complexity and overhead in instrumenting the application and integrating QoS performance metric feedback with many management runtimes. To bridge this gap, we introduced eBeeMetrics, an eBPF-based library framework to accurately observe application-level metrics derived from only eBPF-observable events, such as system calls. eBeeMetrics can be used as a drop-in replacement to decouple system management runtimes from QoS metric feedback reporting, or can supplement existing QoS metrics to better identify server-side dynamics. eBeeMetrics achieves a strong correlation with real-world measured throughput and latency metrics across various latency-sensitive workloads. The eBeeMetrics tool is open-source; the source code is available at: https://github.com/Ibnathism/eBeeMetrics.

</details>


### [10] [DFLOP: A Data-driven Framework for Multimodal LLM Training Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25120)
*Hyeonjun An,Sihyun Kim,Chaerim Lim,Hyunjoon Kim,Rathijit Sen,Sangmin Jung,Hyeonsoo Lee,Dongwook Kim,Takki Yu,Jinkyu Jeong,Youngsok Kim,Kwanghyun Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DFLOP，一个数据驱动的多模态大语言模型训练流水线优化框架，通过感知输入数据特征来解决计算倾斜问题，相比现有方法实现了最高3.6倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式训练框架在并行化计算时忽略了输入数据特征的差异，导致不同模态输入产生不同的处理开销，引发严重的计算倾斜、GPU资源利用率不均、同步延迟累积以及整体训练效率下降。

Method: 提出DFLOP框架，通过持续分析运行时行为来捕获数据引起的计算差异，并采用预测性调度来平衡各阶段和微批次之间的工作负载，将数据特征与执行规划相结合。

Result: 在大规模多模态基准测试上的广泛实验表明，DFLOP相比最先进的分布式训练框架实现了高达3.6倍的训练加速，显著提升了GPU利用率和吞吐量。

Conclusion: DFLOP证明了数据感知调度对于优化多模态大语言模型训练的重要性，为解决异构输入导致的计算不平衡问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable advances by integrating text, image, and audio understanding within a unified architecture. However, existing distributed training frameworks remain fundamentally data-blind: they parallelize computation without accounting for variations in input data characteristics. This data unawareness leads to severe computation skew across stages and microbatches, where heterogeneous multimodal inputs incur different processing costs. Consequently, GPU resources are unevenly utilized, synchronization delays accumulate, and overall training efficiency degrades. To address this limitation, we present DFLOP, a data-driven framework for multimodal LLM training pipeline optimization. DFLOP continuously profiles runtime behavior to capture data-induced computation variance and employs predictive scheduling to balance workloads across stages and microbatches. By coupling data characteristics with execution planning, DFLOP substantially improves GPU utilization and throughput. Extensive experiments on large-scale multimodal benchmarks show that DFLOP achieves up to 3.6x faster training compared to state-of-the-art distributed training frameworks.

</details>


### [11] [Revealing the influence of participant failures on model quality in cross-silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25289)
*Fabian Stricker,David Bermbach,Christian Zirpins*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了联邦学习中参与者缺失（如崩溃故障、网络分区）对模型可靠性的影响，发现数据偏斜是关键因素，常导致模型评估过度乐观并改变其他因素的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在生产环境中的应用需要可靠性保障，但作为分布式系统，其易受崩溃故障、网络分区等影响。目前关于此类故障如何影响联邦学习结果尚缺乏系统性研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在图像、表格和时间序列数据上进行了大量实验，分析参与者缺失对模型性能的影响。考虑了数据偏斜、不同可用性模式和模型架构等影响因素，并考察了全局模型对缺失参与者的效用等场景特定方面。

Result: 实验提供了各影响因素的详细见解。特别发现数据偏斜影响强烈，常导致模型评估过度乐观，某些情况下甚至会改变其他影响因素的效果。参与者缺失会损害学习结果的有效性、稳定性和可复现性。

Conclusion: 可靠性是联邦学习生产部署的关键要求，参与者缺失会显著影响模型性能。数据偏斜是需要重点关注的因素，研究为提升联邦学习系统的可靠性提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is a paradigm for training machine learning (ML) models in collaborative settings while preserving participants' privacy by keeping raw data local. A key requirement for the use of FL in production is reliability, as insufficient reliability can compromise the validity, stability, and reproducibility of learning outcomes. FL inherently operates as a distributed system and is therefore susceptible to crash failures, network partitioning, and other fault scenarios. Despite this, the impact of such failures on FL outcomes has not yet been studied systematically.
  In this paper, we address this gap by investigating the impact of missing participants in FL. To this end, we conduct extensive experiments on image, tabular, and time-series data and analyze how the absence of participants affects model performance, taking into account influencing factors such as data skewness, different availability patterns, and model architectures. Furthermore, we examine scenario-specific aspects, including the utility of the global model for missing participants. Our experiments provide detailed insights into the effects of various influencing factors. In particular, we show that data skewness has a strong impact, often leading to overly optimistic model evaluations and, in some cases, even altering the effects of other influencing factors.

</details>


### [12] [The Complexity of Distributed Minimum Weight Cycle Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25368)
*Yi-Jun Chang,Yanyu Chen,Dipan Dey,Yonggang Jiang,Gopinath Mishra,Hung Thuan Nguyen,Mingyang Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在CONGEST模型下研究了最小权环问题，给出了近似比与轮复杂度之间的平滑权衡，并在小直径图上证明了近乎紧确界的上下界匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小权环问题是图论中的基本问题，在分布式计算模型下的复杂度研究尚不充分。先前的工作（Manoharan和Ramachandran, PODC 2024）仅给出了(2+ε)-近似算法，且下界结果较弱。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索近似比与轮复杂度之间的精确权衡关系。

Method: 作者设计了随机化分布式算法，通过参数k（实数，k≥1）实现(k+1)-近似，算法复杂度涉及节点数n、无权直径D和参数k。下界证明基于Erdős围长猜想，构造特定图例证明对任意整数k≥1，(k+1-ε)-近似算法所需的轮数下界。

Result: 上界：对任意实数k≥1，(k+1)-近似算法轮复杂度为\tilde{O}(n^{(k+1)/(2k+1)} + n^{1/k} + D·n^{1/[2(2k+1)]} + D^{2/5}·n^{2/5+1/[2(2k+1)]})。当k≥2且D=\tilde{O}(n^{1/4})时，简化为\tilde{O}(n^{(k+1)/(2k+1)})。下界：假设Erdős围长猜想，对任意整数k≥1，(k+1-ε)-近似算法需要\tilde{Ω}(n^{(k+1)/(2k+1)})轮，适用于有向无权图和无向带权图，即使直径D=Θ(log n)。上下界在小直径图上（D=\tilde{O}(n^{1/4}), k≥2）几乎紧确匹配（仅相差多对数因子）。

Conclusion: 本文显著推进了分布式最小权环问题的研究，首次给出了近似比与轮复杂度之间的平滑权衡，并在重要参数范围内证明了近乎紧确的上下界匹配，改进了先前的技术水平。

Abstract: We investigate the \emph{minimum weight cycle (MWC)} problem in the $\mathsf{CONGEST}$ model of distributed computing.
  For undirected weighted graphs, we design a randomized algorithm that achieves a $(k+1)$-approximation, for any \emph{real} number $k \ge 1$. The round complexity of algorithm is \[ \tilde{O}\!\Big( n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} + n^{\frac{1}{k}} + D\, n^{\frac{1}{2(2k+1)}} + D^{\frac{2}{5}} n^{\frac{2}{5}+\frac{1}{2(2k+1)}} \Big). \] where $n$ denotes the number of nodes and $D$ is the unweighted diameter of the graph. This result yields a smooth trade-off between approximation ratio and round complexity. In particular, when $k \geq 2$ and $D = \tilde{O}(n^{1/4})$, the bound simplifies to \[ \tilde{O}\!\left(
  n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} \right) \]
  On the lower bound side, assuming the Erdős girth conjecture, we prove that for every \emph{integer} $k \ge 1$, any randomized $(k+1-ε)$-approximation algorithm for MWC requires \[ \tildeΩ\!\left(
  n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} \right) \] rounds. This lower bound holds for both directed unweighted and undirected weighted graphs, and applies even to graphs with small diameter $D = Θ(\log n)$.
  Taken together, our upper and lower bounds \emph{match up to polylogarithmic factors} for graphs of sufficiently small diameter $D = \tilde{O}(n^{1/4})$ (when $k \geq 2$), yielding a nearly tight bound on the distributed complexity of the problem.
  Our results improve upon the previous state of the art: Manoharan and Ramachandran (PODC~2024) demonstrated a $(2+ε)$-approximation algorithm for undirected weighted graphs with round complexity $\tilde{O}(n^{2/3}+D)$, and proved that for any arbitrarily large number $α$, any $α$-approximation algorithm for directed unweighted or undirected weighted graphs requires $Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ rounds.

</details>


### [13] [PRISM: Dynamic Primitive-Based Forecasting for Large-Scale GPU Cluster Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25378)
*Xin Wu,Fei Teng,Xingwang Li,Bin Zheng,Qiang Duan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了PRISM框架，通过基于原语的组合预测方法，显著提升了高度波动和多周期性GPU工作负载的预测准确性，尤其是降低了突发阶段的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU工作负载具有高度波动性、多周期性和异构性，传统预测器难以准确预测，而准确的预测对于AI基础设施的调度、资源分配和功率管理至关重要。

Method: 提出了PRISM框架，这是一种基于原语的组合预测方法。它结合了字典驱动的时间分解和自适应频谱细化，通过双重表示提取跨不同GPU作业的稳定、可解释的工作负载特征。

Result: 在大规模生产追踪数据上评估，PRISM取得了最先进的结果，显著降低了突发阶段的预测误差。

Conclusion: PRISM为GPU驱动的AI平台中的动态资源管理提供了一个稳健的、具备架构感知能力的基础。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting GPU workloads is essential for AI infrastructure, enabling efficient scheduling, resource allocation, and power management. Modern workloads are highly volatile, multiple periodicity, and heterogeneous, making them challenging for traditional predictors. We propose PRISM, a primitive-based compositional forecasting framework combining dictionary-driven temporal decomposition with adaptive spectral refinement. This dual representation extracts stable, interpretable workload signatures across diverse GPU jobs. Evaluated on large-scale production traces, PRISM achieves state-of-the-art results. It significantly reduces burst-phase errors, providing a robust, architecture-aware foundation for dynamic resource management in GPU-powered AI platforms.

</details>


### [14] [SHADOW: Seamless Handoff And Zero-Downtime Orchestrated Workload Migration for Stateful Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25484)
*Hai Dinh-Tuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SHADOW框架，通过ShadowPod策略和身份交换机制，实现了Kubernetes中有状态微服务的零停机迁移，将恢复阶段缩短92%，总迁移时间减少77%，并确保零消息丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes中有状态微服务的迁移面临严峻挑战：容器重启会导致内存状态丢失，而StatefulSet的身份约束禁止相同序号的Pod同时运行，迫使进行串行的停止-重建循环，导致中位数38.5秒的服务停机时间。

Method: 提出SHADOW框架，实现基于消息的有状态微服务迁移(MS2M)方法作为Kubernetes Operator。核心是ShadowPod策略：在目标节点上从CRIU检查点镜像创建影子Pod，同时源Pod继续服务，在消息回放期间实现并发操作。对于StatefulSet工作负载，采用带有ExchangeFence机制的身份交换流程，重新检查点影子Pod，创建StatefulSet拥有的替代Pod，并排空两个消息队列以确保切换期间零消息丢失。

Result: 在裸机Kubernetes集群上进行了280次迁移实验，涵盖四种配置和七种消息速率(10-120msg/s)。与StatefulSet工作负载的串行基准相比，ShadowPod策略将恢复阶段缩短高达92%，完全消除了服务停机时间，将总迁移时间减少高达77%，所有280次运行均实现零消息丢失。

Conclusion: SHADOW框架成功解决了Kubernetes中StatefulSet工作负载迁移的停机问题，通过创新的ShadowPod策略和身份交换机制实现了无缝的零停机迁移，显著提升了迁移效率和服务连续性，为有状态微服务的动态迁移提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Migrating stateful microservices in Kubernetes requires careful state management because in-memory state is lost when a container restarts. For StatefulSet-managed workloads, the problem is compounded by identity constraints that prohibit two pods with the same ordinal from running simultaneously, forcing a sequential stop-recreate cycle with a median 38.5s of service downtime. This paper presents SHADOW Seamless Handoff And Zero-Downtime Orchestrated Workload Migration, a Kubernetes-native framework that implements the Message-based Stateful Microservice Migration (MS2M) approach as a Kubernetes Operator. SHADOW introduces the ShadowPod strategy, where a shadow pod is created from a CRIU checkpoint image on the target node while the source pod continues serving traffic, allowing concurrent operation during message replay. For StatefulSet workloads, an identity swap procedure with the ExchangeFence mechanism re-checkpoints the shadow pod, creates a StatefulSet-owned replacement, and drains both message queues to guarantee zero message loss during the handoff. An evaluation on a bare-metal Kubernetes cluster with 280 migration runs across four configurations and seven message rates (10--120msg/s) shows that, compared to the sequential baseline on the same StatefulSet workload, the ShadowPod strategy reduces the restore phase by up to 92%, eliminates service downtime entirely, and reduces total migration time by up to 77%, with zero message loss across all 280 runs.

</details>

### [15] [ARC-AGI-3: A New Challenge for Frontier Agentic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24621)
*ARC Prize Foundation*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ARC-AGI-3基准，用于评估智能体在新颖抽象环境中的流体适应能力；人类测试者能解决100%的环境，而前沿AI系统得分低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了研究智能体的智能能力，需要一个能够评估探索、目标推断、环境动力学建模和规划能力的基准，同时避免依赖语言和外部知识。

Method: 设计了一个交互式、回合制的抽象环境基准ARC-AGI-3，仅利用核心知识先验，通过大量人类测试者进行难度校准，并提出了基于人类行为基线的效率评分框架。

Result: 人类测试者能够解决100%的环境任务，而截至2026年3月的前沿AI系统在该基准上的得分低于1%。

Conclusion: ARC-AGI-3成功构建了一个能有效区分人类与当前AI系统在流体适应智能方面差距的基准，为未来智能体研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: We introduce ARC-AGI-3, an interactive benchmark for studying agentic intelligence through novel, abstract, turn-based environments in which agents must explore, infer goals, build internal models of environment dynamics, and plan effective action sequences without explicit instructions. Like its predecessors ARC-AGI-1 and 2, ARC-AGI-3 focuses entirely on evaluating fluid adaptive efficiency on novel tasks, while avoiding language and external knowledge. ARC-AGI-3 environments only leverage Core Knowledge priors and are difficulty-calibrated via extensive testing with human test-takers. Our testing shows humans can solve 100% of the environments, in contrast to frontier AI systems which, as of March 2026, score below 1%. In this paper, we present the benchmark design, its efficiency-based scoring framework grounded in human action baselines, and the methodology used to construct, validate, and calibrate the environments.

</details>


### [16] [When Is Collective Intelligence a Lottery? Multi-Agent Scaling Laws for Memetic Drift in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24676)
*Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了QSG模型，揭示了多智能体系统中的“模因漂移”机制：智能体会通过相互的上下文学习将随机选择放大为群体共识。研究发现存在一个临界点，系统会从随机漂移主导（共识如彩票般随机）转变为选择主导（微弱偏差被放大），并推导出了预测极化程度的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统被广泛部署并影响重要决策，其决策结果究竟是源于集体推理、系统性偏差还是纯属偶然尚不明确。近期关于命名博弈的研究进一步凸显了这一问题：即使单个智能体对标签无先验偏好，群体仍能迅速打破对称性并达成共识。本文旨在揭示这一现象背后的微观机制。

Method: 1. 提出一个最小化模型——量化单纯形流言模型（Quantized Simplex Gossip, QSG），用于模拟智能体在维护内部信念状态的同时，通过采样输出相互学习的过程。
2. 追踪共识达成的微观起源，识别出“相互上下文学习”的关键作用。
3. 通过类比中性进化理论，提出“模因漂移”概念描述采样驱动机制。
4. 在QSG模拟和LLM群体的命名博弈实验中验证理论预测。

Result: 1. 揭示了共识达成的微观机制：一个智能体的任意选择成为下一个智能体的证据，并通过相互上下文学习复合形成共识。
2. 发现了两种机制的竞争：漂移主导机制（共识是随机彩票）与选择机制（微弱偏差被放大并塑造结果）。
3. 推导出了漂移诱导极化的标度律，该定律是群体规模、通信带宽、上下文适应率和智能体内部不确定性的函数，并在仿真和LLM实验中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个研究多智能体系统中社会表征形成集体机制的框架。研究结果表明，在缺乏强先验偏好的情况下，LLM多智能体系统的共识可能并非理性推理的结果，而是源于随机采样被放大的“模因漂移”。这为理解和预测多智能体系统的集体行为提供了理论基础，对于部署涉及重要决策的AI系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings that shape consequential decisions, both directly and indirectly. Yet it remains unclear whether their outcomes reflect collective reasoning, systematic bias, or mere chance. Recent work has sharpened this question with naming games, showing that even when no individual agent favors any label a priori, populations rapidly break symmetry and reach consensus. Here, we reveal the mechanism by introducing a minimal model, Quantized Simplex Gossip (QSG), and trace the microscopic origin of this agreement to mutual in-context learning. In QSG, agents maintain internal belief states but learn from one another's sampled outputs, so one agent's arbitrary choice becomes the next agent's evidence and can compound toward agreement. By analogy with neutral evolution, we call this sampling-driven regime memetic drift. QSG predicts a crossover from a drift-dominated regime, where consensus is effectively a lottery, to a selection regime, where weak biases are amplified and shape the outcome. We derive scaling laws for drift-induced polarization as a function of population size, communication bandwidth, in-context adaptation rate, and agents' internal uncertainty, and we validate them in both QSG simulations and naming-game experiments with LLM populations. Together, these results provide a framework for studying the collective mechanisms of social representation formation in multi-agent systems.

Abstract: In the design and safety analysis of advanced reactor systems, constructing input files for system-level thermal-hydraulics codes such as the System Analysis Module (SAM) remains a labor-intensive task. Analysts must extract and reconcile design data from heterogeneous engineering documents and manually translate it into solver-specific syntax. In this paper, we present AutoSAM, an agentic framework that automates SAM input file generation. The framework combines a large language model agent with retrieval-augmented generation over the solver's user guide and theory manual, together with specialized tools for analyzing PDFs, images, spreadsheets, and text files. AutoSAM ingests unstructured engineering documents, including system diagrams, design reports, and data tables, extracts simulation-relevant parameters into a human-auditable intermediate representation, and synthesizes validated, solver-compatible input decks. Its multimodal retrieval pipeline integrates scientific text extraction, vision-based figure interpretation, semantic embedding, and query answering. We evaluate AutoSAM on four case studies of increasing complexity: a single-pipe steady-state model, a solid-fuel channel with temperature reactivity feedback, the Advanced Burner Test Reactor core, and the Molten Salt Reactor Experiment primary loop. Across all cases, the agent produces runnable SAM models consistent with expected thermal-hydraulic behavior while explicitly identifying missing data and labeling assumed values. The framework achieves 100% utilization of structured inputs, about 88% extraction from PDF text, and 100% completeness in vision-based geometric extraction. These results demonstrate a practical path toward prompt-driven reactor modeling, in which analysts provide system descriptions and supporting documentation while the agent translates them into transparent, and executable, SAM simulations.

</details>

Method: 提出Agent Factory两阶段流水线：第一阶段将设计分解为子核，独立优化并利用整数线性规划（ILP）在面积约束下组合全局配置；第二阶段启动N个专家智能体探索跨函数优化（如pragma重组、循环融合、内存重构）。使用Claude Code（Opus 4.5/4.6）和AMD Vitis HLS进行评估。

Abstract: We present an empirical study of how far general-purpose coding agents -- without hardware-specific training -- can optimize hardware designs from high-level algorithmic specifications. We introduce an agent factory, a two-stage pipeline that constructs and coordinates multiple autonomous optimization agents.
  In Stage~1, the pipeline decomposes a design into sub-kernels, independently optimizes each using pragma and code-level transformations, and formulates an Integer Linear Program (ILP) to assemble globally promising configurations under an area constraint. In Stage~2, it launches $N$ expert agents over the top ILP solutions, each exploring cross-function optimizations such as pragma recombination, loop fusion, and memory restructuring that are not captured by sub-kernel decomposition.
  We evaluate the approach on 12 kernels from HLS-Eval and Rodinia-HLS using Claude Code (Opus~4.5/4.6) with AMD Vitis HLS. Scaling from 1 to 10 agents yields a mean $8.27\times$ speedup over baseline, with larger gains on harder benchmarks: streamcluster exceeds $20\times$ and kmeans reaches approximately $10\times$. Across benchmarks, agents consistently rediscover known hardware optimization patterns without domain-specific training, and the best designs often do not originate from top-ranked ILP candidates, indicating that global optimization exposes improvements missed by sub-kernel search. These results establish agent scaling as a practical and effective axis for HLS optimization.

</details>


### [19] [Trust as Monitoring: Evolutionary Dynamics of User Trust and AI Developer Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24742)
*Adeela Bashir,Zhao Song,Ndidi Bianca Ogbo,Nataliya Balabanova,Martin Smit,Chin-wing Leung,Paolo Bova,Manuel Chica Serrano,Dhanushka Dissanayake,Manh Hong Duong,Elias Fernandez Domingos,Nikita Huber-Kralj,Marcus Krellner,Andrew Powell,Stefan Sarkadi,Fernando P. Santos,Zia Ush Shamszaman,Chaimaa Tarzi,Paolo Turrini,Grace Ibukunoluwa Ufeoshi,Victor A. Vargas-Perez,Alessandro Di Stefano,Simon T. Powers,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用演化博弈论研究AI安全治理，发现只有当惩罚力度超过安全成本且用户仍能负担偶尔监控时，才能实现安全且广泛应用的理想结果。这支持了强调透明度、低成本监控和实质性制裁的治理提案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI能力的增长，AI安全性日益紧迫。现有的AI治理演化模型通常将用户信任表示为一次性的采纳选择，忽略了信任是在重复互动中动态演化的过程，且受到监控成本的影响。

Method: 将信任建模为用户与AI开发者之间重复非对称互动中监控的减少（检查AI行为具有成本）。利用演化博弈论研究用户信任策略与开发者安全/不安全选择如何在不同监控成本和制度环境下共同演化。结合了无限种群复制子分析、随机有限种群动力学和强化学习（Q-learning）模拟。

Result: 发现了三种稳健的长期演化体制：无采纳且不安全开发、不安全但广泛采纳的系统、安全且广泛采纳的系统。只有最后一种是理想的，它出现在对不安全行为的惩罚超过安全的额外成本，且用户仍能负担至少偶尔监控的情况下。

Conclusion: 研究结果正式支持了强调透明度、低成本监控和实质性制裁的治理提案。单纯的监管或盲目的用户信任都不足以防止演化漂移向不安全或低采纳的结果，必须结合有效的惩罚机制和可负担的用户监控。

Abstract: AI safety is an increasingly urgent concern as the capabilities and adoption of AI systems grow. Existing evolutionary models of AI governance have primarily examined incentives for safe development and effective regulation, typically representing users' trust as a one-shot adoption choice rather than as a dynamic, evolving process shaped by repeated interactions. We instead model trust as reduced monitoring in a repeated, asymmetric interaction between users and AI developers, where checking AI behaviour is costly. Using evolutionary game theory, we study how user trust strategies and developer choices between safe (compliant) and unsafe (non-compliant) AI co-evolve under different levels of monitoring cost and institutional regimes. We complement the infinite-population replicator analysis with stochastic finite-population dynamics and reinforcement learning (Q-learning) simulations. Across these approaches, we find three robust long-run regimes: no adoption with unsafe development, unsafe but widely adopted systems, and safe systems that are widely adopted. Only the last is desirable, and it arises when penalties for unsafe behaviour exceed the extra cost of safety and users can still afford to monitor at least occasionally. Our results formally support governance proposals that emphasise transparency, low-cost monitoring, and meaningful sanctions, and they show that neither regulation alone nor blind user trust is sufficient to prevent evolutionary drift towards unsafe or low-adoption outcomes.

</details>


### [20] [Formal Semantics for Agentic Tool Protocols: A Process Calculus Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24747)
*Andreas Schlapbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了SGD和MCP的进程演算形式化建模，证明了二者在映射Φ下结构互似，但反向映射有损；据此提出MCP+类型系统扩展，实现与SGD的行为等价，为验证智能体系统奠定了形式化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着能够调用外部工具的大语言模型智能体的出现，对智能体协议进行形式化验证的需求日益迫切。SGD（学术框架）和MCP（工业标准）是两大主流范式，但两者之间的形式化关系尚未被探索。

Method: 首次使用进程演算对SGD和MCP进行形式化建模，定义映射Φ证明二者结构互似，并进行双向分析揭示反向映射的不完备性。基于分析结果，提出五项核心原则并将其形式化为类型系统扩展MCP+。

Result: 证明了SGD与MCP在映射Φ下结构互似，但反向映射Φ^{-1}是部分的且有损，揭示了MCP在表达性上的关键缺口。通过引入MCP+类型系统扩展，证明了MCP+与SGD同构，且schema质量可作为可证明的安全属性。

Conclusion: 本研究为验证智能体系统提供了首个形式化基础，明确了实现SGD与MCP完全行为等价的必要充分条件，并将schema质量确立为可证明的安全属性，为智能体协议的设计与验证提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The emergence of large language model agents capable of invoking external tools has created urgent need for formal verification of agent protocols. Two paradigms dominate this space: Schema-Guided Dialogue (SGD), a research framework for zero-shot API generalization, and the Model Context Protocol (MCP), an industry standard for agent-tool integration. While both enable dynamic service discovery through schema descriptions, their formal relationship remains unexplored. Building on prior work establishing the conceptual convergence of these paradigms, we present the first process calculus formalization of SGD and MCP, proving they are structurally bisimilar under a well-defined mapping Phi. However, we demonstrate that the reverse mapping Phi^{-1} is partial and lossy, revealing critical gaps in MCP's expressivity. Through bidirectional analysis, we identify five principles -- semantic completeness, explicit action boundaries, failure mode documentation, progressive disclosure compatibility, and inter-tool relationship declaration -- as necessary and sufficient conditions for full behavioral equivalence. We formalize these principles as type-system extensions MCP+, proving MCP+ is isomorphic to SGD. Our work provides the first formal foundation for verified agent systems and establishes schema quality as a provable safety property.

</details>


### [21] [Supervising Ralph Wiggum: Exploring a Metacognitive Co-Regulation Agentic AI Loop for Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24768)
*Zeda Xu,Nikolas Martelaro,Christopher McComb*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同元认知调节系统（CRDAL），通过引入独立的元认知辅助代理来缓解LLM设计代理的设计固着问题，在电池包设计任务中显著提升了设计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的工程设计智能体系统存在与人类设计师相似的"设计固着"问题——倾向于固守现有范式而未能充分探索替代方案，可能导致次优解。作者受到人类认知过程中元认知调节机制的启发，试图通过引入元认知监控机制来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了两种新型智能体架构：(1) 自调节循环（SRL）：设计代理自身进行元认知监控和自我调节；(2) 协同调节设计智能体循环（CRDAL）：引入一个独立的元认知协同调节代理来辅助设计代理进行元认知活动。通过电池包设计问题进行实验验证，并与基线的Ralph Wiggum循环（RWL）和SRL进行对比。

Result: CRDAL系统生成了性能更优的设计方案，且未显著增加计算成本；CRDAL在潜在设计空间中的导航能力优于SRL和RWL；SRL探索了不同的设计空间区域，但其设计性能并未显著优于RWL基线。

Conclusion: 协同调节架构（CRDAL）能有效缓解设计固着问题并提升工程设计任务性能，而单纯的自调节机制（SRL）虽能改变探索行为但不足以提升性能。该研究为未来工程设计智能体系统的开发提供了实用的架构指导和理论依据。

Abstract: The engineering design research community has studied agentic AI systems that use Large Language Model (LLM) agents to automate the engineering design process. However, these systems are prone to some of the same pathologies that plague humans. Just as human designers, LLM design agents can fixate on existing paradigms and fail to explore alternatives when solving design challenges, potentially leading to suboptimal solutions. In this work, we propose (1) a novel Self-Regulation Loop (SRL), in which the Design Agent self-regulates and explicitly monitors its own metacognition, and (2) a novel Co-Regulation Design Agentic Loop (CRDAL), in which a Metacognitive Co-Regulation Agent assists the Design Agent in metacognition to mitigate design fixation, thereby improving system performance for engineering design tasks. In the battery pack design problem examined here, we found that the novel CRDAL system generates designs with better performance, without significantly increasing the computational cost, compared to a plain Ralph Wiggum Loop (RWL) and the metacognitively self-assessing Self-Regulation Loop (SRL). Also, we found that the CRDAL system navigated through the latent design space more effectively than both SRL and RWL. However, the SRL did not generate designs with significantly better performance than RWL, even though it explored a different region of the design space. The proposed system architectures and findings of this work provide practical implications for future development of agentic AI systems for engineering design.

</details>


### [22] [ReLope: KL-Regularized LoRA Probes for Multimodal LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24787)
*Yaopei Zeng,Congchao Wang,Blake JianHang Chen,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)中探针路由性能下降的问题，提出了Attention Probe和KL-Regularized LoRA Probe (ReLope)两种互补方法，显著提升了路由效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的探针路由方法在纯文本大语言模型中表现良好，但在多模态大语言模型中性能显著下降。研究发现视觉输入的引入削弱了隐藏状态中正确性信号的可分离性，使得标准探针设计难以提取这些信号。

Method: 提出了两种互补的方法：(1) Attention Probe：基于注意力分数聚合前一层的隐藏状态，以恢复分布式的正确性信号；(2) KL-Regularized LoRA Probe (ReLope)：插入轻量级LoRA适配器并应用KL正则化项，学习路由感知的表示。

Result: 在综合实验中，所提出的方法持续优于基线方法，证明了提升隐藏状态质量是多模态大语言模型有效路由的关键。

Conclusion: 通过改善隐藏状态的质量，可以有效解决多模态大语言模型中探针路由性能下降的问题，为MLLMs中的性能-成本平衡提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Routing has emerged as a promising strategy for balancing performance and cost in large language model (LLM) systems that combine lightweight models with powerful but expensive large models. Recent studies show that \emph{probe routing}, which predicts the correctness of a small model using its hidden states, provides an effective solution in text-only LLMs. However, we observe that these probes degrade substantially when applied to multimodal LLMs (MLLMs). Through empirical analysis, we find that the presence of visual inputs weakens the separability of correctness signals in hidden states, making them harder to extract using standard probe designs. To address this challenge, we introduce two complementary approaches for improving probe routing in MLLMs. First, we propose the \emph{Attention Probe}, which aggregates hidden states from the preceding layer based on attention scores to recover distributed correctness signals. Second, we present the \emph{KL-Regularized LoRA Probe (ReLope)}, which inserts a lightweight LoRA adapter and applies a KL regularizer to learn routing-aware representations. Comprehensive experiments show that our methods consistently outperform baselines, suggesting that improving the quality of hidden states is key to effective routing in MLLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/Spinozaaa/ReLope.

</details>


### [23] [Resisting Humanization: Ethical Front-End Design Choices in AI for Sensitive Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24853)
*Silvia Rossi,Diletta Huyskes,Mackenzie Jorgensen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出AI伦理研究忽视了前端设计的选择，通过分析Chayn案例，论证了前端人性化设计是价值驱动的选择，会深刻影响用户的心理模型、信任校准和行为反应，主张应实施审慎的前端设计伦理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI伦理辩论主要关注后端问题（数据治理、模型训练、算法决策），而忽视了前端设计选择的伦理意义。对于基于NLP的对话式用户界面(CUI)，人性化设计元素（对话交互、情感语言、个性模式、拟人化隐喻）日益普遍，这一空白尤为显著。研究者希望探讨前端设计如何影响用户期望、信任和自主性。

Method: 结合人机交互(HCI)、对话式AI和价值敏感设计的研究成果，分析界面如何在用户期望错位、错误信任和削弱用户自主性方面发挥核心作用。以支持性别暴力幸存者的非营利组织Chayn开发的两个AI系统为案例研究，展示其如何将创伤知情设计原则应用于AI开发中。

Result: 研究发现前端人性化设计是价值驱动的选择，深刻塑造用户的心理模型、信任校准和行为反应。Chayn案例展示了如何通过创伤知情设计原则实施谨慎的界面设计，挑战当代AI产品中基于参与度的规范。伦理考量可以激发界面设计中的原则性克制。

Conclusion: 论证了伦理前端AI设计是一种程序伦理，通过交互选择而非仅嵌入系统逻辑来实施。这挑战了当前AI产品中以参与度为导向的设计规范，强调了前端设计选择在AI伦理中的重要地位，特别是在脆弱情境下对用户自主性的保护。

Abstract: Ethical debates in AI have primarily focused on back-end issues such as data governance, model training, and algorithmic decision-making. Less attention has been paid to the ethical significance of front-end design choices, such as the interaction and representation-based elements through which users interact with AI systems. This gap is particularly significant for Conversational User Interfaces (CUI) based on Natural Language Processing (NLP) systems, where humanizing design elements such as dialogue-based interaction, emotive language, personality modes, and anthropomorphic metaphors are increasingly prevalent. This work argues that humanization in AI front-end design is a value-driven choice that profoundly shapes users' mental models, trust calibration, and behavioral responses. Drawing on research in human-computer interaction (HCI), conversational AI, and value-sensitive design, we examine how interfaces can play a central role in misaligning user expectations, fostering misplaced trust, and subtly undermining user autonomy, especially in vulnerable contexts. To ground this analysis, we discuss two AI systems developed by Chayn, a nonprofit organization supporting survivors of gender-based violence. Chayn is extremely cautious when building AI that interacts with or impacts survivors by operationalizing their trauma-informed design principles. This Chayn case study illustrates how ethical considerations can motivate principled restraint in interface design, challenging engagement-based norms in contemporary AI products. We argue that ethical front-end AI design is a form of procedural ethics, enacted through interaction choices rather than embedded solely in system logic.

</details>


### [24] [SentinelAI: A Multi-Agent Framework for Structuring and Linking NG9-1-1 Emergency Incident Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24856)
*Kliment Ho,Ilya Zaslavsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SentinelAI框架，通过可扩展的处理流水线和专用智能体，将应急通信转换为符合NENA标准的机器可读数据集，解决多源数据整合与标准化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有应急响应系统的数据来自多个机构和系统，实践中难以按照下一代9-1-1数据标准进行跨源数据的关联与更新。理想情况下，数据应被视为连续的操作更新流，新事实需立即整合以提供事件演变的及时统一视图。

Method: 提出SentinelAI——一个数据集成与标准化框架，实现可扩展的处理流水线，由专用智能体组成。其中EIDO Agent负责摄取原始通信并生成符合NENA标准的Emergency Incident Data Object JSON。

Result: SentinelAI成功将应急通信转换为标准化的机器可读数据集，支持数据集成、复合事件构建和跨源推理。

Conclusion: 该框架为应急通信数据的标准化与实时集成提供了可行方案，有助于实现事件信息的统一视图与即时更新。

Abstract: Emergency response systems generate data from many agencies and systems. In practice, correlating and updating this information across sources in a way that aligns with Next Generation 9-1-1 data standards remains challenging. Ideally, this data should be treated as a continuous stream of operational updates, where new facts are integrated immediately to provide a timely and unified view of an evolving incident. This paper presents SentinelAI, a data integration and standardization framework for transforming emergency communications into standardized, machine-readable datasets that support integration, composite incident construction, and cross-source reasoning. SentinelAI implements a scalable processing pipeline composed of specialized agents. The EIDO Agent ingests raw communications and produces NENA-compliant Emergency Incident Data Object JSON.

</details>


### [25] [How Far Are Vision-Language Models from Constructing the Real World? A Benchmark for Physical Generative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24866)
*Luyu Yang,Yutong Dai,An Yan,Viraj Prabhu,Ran Xu,Zeyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DreamHouse基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在住宅木结构建筑领域的物理生成推理能力，发现现有模型在结构合理性、可建造性和规范合规性方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型评估过于侧重视觉真实感，忽视了模型对物理世界结构约束、建造过程和依赖关系的理解能力。现有基准很少测试模型是否掌握实际建造所需的逐步过程和物理依赖关系，而这种能力对于自动化设计到建造流程至关重要。

Method: 构建了包含26,000多个住宅木结构建筑的数据集，涵盖13种建筑风格，每个结构都达到施工文档标准（LOD 350）；开发了确定性10项结构验证框架；设计迭代式智能体交互环境，让模型观察中间建造状态、生成建造动作并接收结构化环境反馈，从而细粒度评估规划、结构推理和自我修正能力。

Result: 对最先进的视觉语言模型进行广泛实验，发现模型在物理有效性方面存在显著的能力差距，这些差距在现有排行榜上基本不可见；证明了物理有效性是独立于视觉真实感的关键评估维度。

Conclusion: 物理生成推理是多模态智能中一个独特且发展不足的前沿领域，物理有效性应作为与视觉真实感正交的关键评估轴，现有模型在这一维度上仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: The physical world is not merely visual; it is governed by rigorous structural and procedural constraints. Yet, the evaluation of vision-language models (VLMs) remains heavily skewed toward perceptual realism, prioritizing the generation of visually plausible 3D layouts, shapes, and appearances. Current benchmarks rarely test whether models grasp the step-by-step processes and physical dependencies required to actually build these artifacts, a capability essential for automating design-to-construction pipelines. To address this, we introduce DreamHouse, a novel benchmark for physical generative reasoning: the capacity to synthesize artifacts that concurrently satisfy geometric, structural, constructability, and code-compliance constraints. We ground this benchmark in residential timber-frame construction, a domain with fully codified engineering standards and objectively verifiable correctness. We curate over 26,000 structures spanning 13 architectural styles, ach verified to construction-document standards (LOD 350) and develop a deterministic 10-test structural validation framework. Unlike static benchmarks that assess only final outputs, DreamHouse supports iterative agentic interaction. Models observe intermediate build states, generate construction actions, and receive structured environmental feedback, enabling a fine-grained evaluation of planning, structural reasoning, and self-correction. Extensive experiments with state-of-the-art VLMs reveal substantial capability gaps that are largely invisible on existing leaderboards. These findings establish physical validity as a critical evaluation axis orthogonal to visual realism, highlighting physical generative reasoning as a distinct and underdeveloped frontier in multimodal intelligence. Available at https://luluyuyuyang.github.io/dreamhouse

</details>


### [26] [On the Foundations of Trustworthy Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24904)
*TJ Dunham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 证明了平台确定性推理是实现可信AI的充要条件，通过纯整数推理引擎实现了跨架构的逐位一致输出，将AI信任问题转化为算术问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统依赖IEEE 754浮点运算，导致跨平台推理结果不确定，严重阻碍了AI系统的可验证性与可信度，而公平性、鲁棒性、隐私、安全性和对齐等所有主要信任属性都隐含假设了平台确定性。

Method: 提出确定性论题并引入信任熵量化非确定性代价，形式化证明验证失败概率等于1-2^{-H_T}；构造纯整数推理引擎替代浮点运算，在ARM和x86架构上实现逐位一致输出。

Result: 在多达6.7B参数模型的82项跨架构测试中观察到零哈希不匹配；四个地理分布式节点产生完全相同的输出，并通过356笔链上认证交易验证；系统由99,000行Rust代码实现，部署于三大洲。

Conclusion: 平台确定性推理是可信AI的必要且充分条件；在确定性条件下验证仅需O(1)哈希比较，无确定性则面临不可解的成员问题；AI信任本质上是一个算术问题。

Abstract: We prove that platform-deterministic inference is necessary and sufficient for
  trustworthy AI. We formalize this as the Determinism Thesis and introduce trust
  entropy to quantify the cost of non-determinism, proving that verification failure
  probability equals 1 - 2^{-H_T} exactly. We prove a Determinism-Verification
  Collapse: verification under determinism requires O(1) hash comparison; without it,
  the verifier faces an intractable membership problem. IEEE 754 floating-point
  arithmetic fundamentally violates the determinism requirement. We resolve this by
  constructing a pure integer inference engine that achieves bitwise identical output
  across ARM and x86. In 82 cross-architecture tests on models up to 6.7B parameters,
  we observe zero hash mismatches. Four geographically distributed nodes produce
  identical outputs, verified by 356 on-chain attestation transactions. Every major
  trust property of AI systems (fairness, robustness, privacy, safety, alignment)
  presupposes platform determinism. Our system, 99,000 lines of Rust deployed across
  three continents, establishes that AI trust is a question of arithmetic.

</details>


### [27] [LogitScope: A Framework for Analyzing LLM Uncertainty Through Information Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24929)
*Farhan Ahmed,Yuya Jeremy Ong,Chad DeLuca*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了LogitScope，一个轻量级、模型无关的框架，通过计算token级别的信息指标（如熵和变熵）来分析大语言模型的生成不确定性，能有效识别潜在幻觉和高不确定决策点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估方法难以深入洞察大语言模型在生成过程中每个token位置的具体置信度，缺乏对模型输出不确定性的细粒度理解，这阻碍了模型的可靠部署。

Method: 引入LogitScope框架，通过计算生成过程中每一步概率分布的信息指标（如熵entropy和变熵varentropy）来量化token级别的不确定性；该框架采用惰性求值以保证计算效率，并兼容任意HuggingFace模型。

Result: 该方法无需标注数据或语义解释，即可揭示模型置信度模式、识别潜在幻觉并暴露模型表现出高不确定性的决策点；在不确定性量化、模型行为分析和生产监控等多种应用中展示了其有效性。

Conclusion: LogitScope为研究人员和从业者提供了一个计算高效且通用的工具，能够在大语言模型推理过程中进行细粒度的行为检查，有助于提升模型部署的可靠性。

Abstract: Understanding and quantifying uncertainty in large language model (LLM) outputs is critical for reliable deployment. However, traditional evaluation approaches provide limited insight into model confidence at individual token positions during generation. To address this issue, we introduce LogitScope, a lightweight framework for analyzing LLM uncertainty through token-level information metrics computed from probability distributions. By measuring metrics such as entropy and varentropy at each generation step, LogitScope reveals patterns in model confidence, identifies potential hallucinations, and exposes decision points where models exhibit high uncertainty, all without requiring labeled data or semantic interpretation. We demonstrate LogitScope's utility across diverse applications including uncertainty quantification, model behavior analysis, and production monitoring. The framework is model-agnostic, computationally efficient through lazy evaluation, and compatible with any HuggingFace model, enabling both researchers and practitioners to inspect LLM behavior during inference.

</details>


### [28] [Decoding Market Emotions in Cryptocurrency Tweets via Predictive Statement Classification with Machine Learning and Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24933)
*Moein Shahiki Tash,Zahra Ahani,Mohim Tash,Mostafa Keikhay Farzaneh,Ari Y. Barrera-Animas,Olga Kolesnikova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个针对加密货币推文预测性陈述的两阶段分类框架，结合GPT辅助标注与数据增强，发现基于GPT的平衡策略显著提升模型性能，并揭示了不同预测类别具有独特的情感模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着加密货币日益普及，社交媒体上的公众参与和投机活动显著增加，亟需一种有效的方法来识别和分类推文中的预测性陈述，以理解公众情绪与市场动态。

Method: 构建了一个两阶段分类框架：任务一进行二分类（预测性与非预测性）；任务二对预测性推文进行细粒度分类（增值、贬值、中性）。数据集结合人工与GPT标注，利用SenticNet提取情感特征，采用GPT生成式改写进行数据增强以解决类别不平衡。评估了机器学习、深度学习和基于Transformer的多种模型。

Result: 基于GPT的数据增强显著提升了模型性能。在任务一中，Transformer模型取得了最高的F1分数；在任务二中，传统机器学习模型表现最佳。情感分析揭示了不同加密货币的各预测类别具有显著不同的情感模式。

Conclusion: 该框架有效识别了加密货币推文中的预测性陈述，证明了GPT辅助标注与数据增强的价值，并通过情感分析为理解加密货币市场的公众情绪提供了新视角。

Abstract: The growing prominence of cryptocurrencies has triggered widespread public engagement and increased speculative activity, particularly on social media platforms. This study introduces a novel classification framework for identifying predictive statements in cryptocurrency-related tweets, focusing on five popular cryptocurrencies: Cardano, Matic, Binance, Ripple, and Fantom. The classification process is divided into two stages: Task 1 involves binary classification to distinguish between Predictive and Non-Predictive statements. Tweets identified as Predictive proceed to Task 2, where they are further categorized as Incremental, Decremental, or Neutral. To build a robust dataset, we combined manual and GPT-based annotation methods and utilized SenticNet to extract emotion features corresponding to each prediction category. To address class imbalance, GPT-generated paraphrasing was employed for data augmentation. We evaluated a wide range of machine learning, deep learning, and transformer-based models across both tasks. The results show that GPT-based balancing significantly enhanced model performance, with transformer models achieving the highest F1-score in Task 1, while traditional machine learning models performed best in Task 2. Furthermore, our emotion analysis revealed distinct emotional patterns associated with each prediction category across the different cryptocurrencies.

</details>


### [29] [FinMCP-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Real-World Financial Tool Use under the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24943)
*Jie Zhu,Yimin Tian,Boyang Li,Kehao Wu,Zhongzhi Liang,Junhui Li,Xianyin Zhang,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Yong Liu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FinMCP-Bench，一个包含613个样本、覆盖10个主要场景和33个子场景的金融大模型评测基准，旨在通过工具调用评估LLM解决现实金融问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融领域的大语言模型评测缺乏对工具调用能力的系统性评估，且难以覆盖真实金融场景的复杂性。为了推动金融LLM智能体的研究与应用，需要一个标准化、实用且具有挑战性的评测基准。

Method: 构建了FinMCP-Bench基准，包含613个样本，涵盖10个主场景和33个子场景，融合真实用户查询与合成查询。集成65个真实金融模型上下文协议（MCP），设计了单工具、多工具和多轮对话三种样本类型以覆盖不同任务复杂度。提出了显式测量工具调用准确性和推理能力的评测指标，并对主流LLM进行了系统性评估。

Result: FinMCP-Bench成功对一系列主流大语言模型进行了系统性评估，能够有效衡量模型在金融工具调用任务中的准确性与推理能力。该基准在样本多样性、场景真实性和任务复杂度方面均表现出色。

Conclusion: FinMCP-Bench为金融领域LLM智能体的研究提供了一个标准化、实用且富有挑战性的评测平台，有助于推动金融大模型在真实场景中工具调用能力的研究与发展。

Abstract: This paper introduces \textbf{FinMCP-Bench}, a novel benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) in solving real-world financial problems through tool invocation of financial model context protocols. FinMCP-Bench contains 613 samples spanning 10 main scenarios and 33 sub-scenarios, featuring both real and synthetic user queries to ensure diversity and authenticity. It incorporates 65 real financial MCPs and three types of samples, single tool, multi-tool, and multi-turn, allowing evaluation of models across different levels of task complexity. Using this benchmark, we systematically assess a range of mainstream LLMs and propose metrics that explicitly measure tool invocation accuracy and reasoning capabilities. FinMCP-Bench provides a standardized, practical, and challenging testbed for advancing research on financial LLM agents.

</details>


### [30] [Shopping with a Platform AI Assistant: Who Adopts, When in the Journey, and What For](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24947)
*Se Yan,Han Zhong,Zemin,Zhong,Wenyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于携程3100万用户数据，首次大规模研究电商嵌入式购物AI的采纳与使用模式。发现采纳者以年长、女性和高活跃用户为主；AI聊天与搜索在购买旅程中交替出现；用户主要将AI用于探索性、难以关键词化的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献主要关注通用AI工具的采纳模式，缺乏对电商场景中嵌入式购物AI大规模使用的实证研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过分析中国最大在线旅游平台携程的LLM助手"问道"，揭示消费者如何采纳和使用平台嵌入式购物AI。

Method: 利用携程平台3100万用户的大规模数据，对嵌入式LLM购物助手"问道"的采纳和使用行为进行描述性实证分析，研究用户特征、使用时机以及与搜索行为的交互模式。

Result: 发现三个实证规律：1）采纳者以年长、女性和高活跃用户为主，颠覆了通用AI年轻男性主导的模式；2）AI聊天与搜索在购买旅程中交替出现，呈交织模式；3）用户主要将AI用于探索性任务（42%为景点查询），聊天意图随时机和产品类别系统变化。

Conclusion: 嵌入式购物AI并非传统搜索的替代品，而是电子商务中探索性产品发现的互补界面，为电商平台AI功能设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: This paper provides some of the first large-scale descriptive evidence on how consumers adopt and use platform-embedded shopping AI in e-commerce. Using data on 31 million users of Ctrip, China's largest online travel platform, we study "Wendao," an LLM-based AI assistant integrated into the platform. We document three empirical regularities. First, adoption is highest among older consumers, female users, and highly engaged existing users, reversing the younger, male-dominated profile commonly documented for general-purpose AI tools. Second, AI chat appears in the same broad phase of the purchase journey as traditional search and well before order placement; among journeys containing both chat and search, the most common pattern is interleaving, with users moving back and forth between the two modalities. Third, consumers disproportionately use the assistant for exploratory, hard-to-keyword tasks: attraction queries account for 42% of observed chat requests, and chat intent varies systematically with both the timing of chat relative to search and the category of products later purchased within the same journey. These findings suggest that embedded shopping AI functions less as a substitute for conventional search than as a complementary interface for exploratory product discovery in e-commerce.

</details>


### [31] [Can MLLMs Read Students' Minds? Unpacking Multimodal Error Analysis in Handwritten Math](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24961)
*Dingjie Song,Tianlong Xu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Hang Li,Zhiling Yan,Xing Fan,Haoyang Li,Lichao Sun,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ScratchMath基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在真实手写数学草稿上的错误解释与分类能力，发现当前模型在视觉识别和逻辑推理上与人类专家存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育NLP研究多聚焦于文本回答，忽视了真实手写草稿的复杂性和多模态特性；当前多模态大语言模型虽擅长视觉推理，但多采用“应试者视角”追求正确答案，而非诊断学生错误，缺乏针对手写草稿错误分析的专用基准。

Method: 构建了ScratchMath基准数据集，包含1720份来自中国中小学生的数学手写草稿样本，定义了七种错误类型，支持错误原因解释（ECE）和错误原因分类（ECC）两项任务，并通过严格的人工与机器协作流程进行多阶段标注与审核；系统评估了16个主流多模态大语言模型在该基准上的表现。

Result: 评估结果显示，所有模型在ScratchMath上的表现与人类专家相比存在显著差距，尤其在视觉识别和逻辑推理方面；闭源模型显著优于开源模型，大型推理模型在错误解释任务上展现出较强潜力。

Conclusion: ScratchMath填补了手写数学草稿错误分析基准的空白，揭示了当前多模态模型在真实教育场景下的不足，为未来研究提供了公开的评估数据与框架，推动个性化教育反馈技术的发展。

Abstract: Assessing student handwritten scratchwork is crucial for personalized educational feedback but presents unique challenges due to diverse handwriting, complex layouts, and varied problem-solving approaches. Existing educational NLP primarily focuses on textual responses and neglects the complexity and multimodality inherent in authentic handwritten scratchwork. Current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at visual reasoning but typically adopt an "examinee perspective", prioritizing generating correct answers rather than diagnosing student errors. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ScratchMath, a novel benchmark specifically designed for explaining and classifying errors in authentic handwritten mathematics scratchwork. Our dataset comprises 1,720 mathematics samples from Chinese primary and middle school students, supporting two key tasks: Error Cause Explanation (ECE) and Error Cause Classification (ECC), with seven defined error types. The dataset is meticulously annotated through rigorous human-machine collaborative approaches involving multiple stages of expert labeling, review, and verification. We systematically evaluate 16 leading MLLMs on ScratchMath, revealing significant performance gaps relative to human experts, especially in visual recognition and logical reasoning. Proprietary models notably outperform open-source models, with large reasoning models showing strong potential for error explanation. All evaluation data and frameworks are publicly available to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [32] [Design Once, Deploy at Scale: Template-Driven ML Development for Large Model Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24963)
*Jiang Liu,John Martabano Landy,Yao Xuan,Swamy Muddu,Nhat Le,Munaf Sahaf,Luc Kien Hang,Rupinder Khandpour,Kevin De Angeli,Chang Yang,Shouyuan Chen,Shiblee Sadik,Ani Agrawal,Djordje Gligorijevic,Jingzheng Qin,Peggy Yao,Alireza Vahdatpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出标准模型模板（SMT）框架，通过标准化、可组合的ML组件构建推荐模型，在Meta广告排名系统中实现了0.63%的交叉熵改进、92%的工程时间减少和6.3倍的技术推广效率提升，证明多样化优化目标无需依赖多样化的模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算广告平台维护大量ML模型以支持多种产品场景和广告主目标，但这种规模带来了显著的效率和开发挑战：模型定期刷新和新技术传播需要大量工程投入，导致ML创新部署延迟。传统观点认为不同优化目标需要定制化模型设计，但这加剧了技术传播复杂性问题。

Method: 提出标准模型模板（SMT）框架，采用标准化、可组合的ML模型组件构建适应不同数据分布和优化事件的高性能模型。研究在Meta生产广告排名生态系统中进行了大规模实证研究，对比了标准化模型构建方法与独立模型优化方法，并在四个全球开发周期中评估了大量模型。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）在中性服务容量下交叉熵平均改进0.63%；（2）单模型迭代工程时间减少92%；（3）技术-模型对采用吞吐量提升6.3倍。SMT将技术传播复杂度从O(n·2^k)降至O(n+k)，其中n为模型数量，k为技术数量。

Conclusion: 研究发现挑战了"多样化优化目标本质上需要多样化ML模型设计"的传统观念，证明标准化方法可以显著提升模型性能、开发效率和技术推广速度。SMT框架成功实现了在保持高性能的同时大幅降低工程复杂度，为大规模推荐系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Modern computational advertising platforms typically rely on recommendation systems to predict user responses, such as click-through rates, conversion rates, and other optimization events. To support a wide variety of product surfaces and advertiser goals, these platforms frequently maintain an extensive ecosystem of machine learning (ML) models. However, operating at this scale creates significant development and efficiency challenges. Substantial engineering effort is required to regularly refresh ML models and propagate new techniques, which results in long latencies when deploying ML innovations across the ecosystem.
  We present a large-scale empirical study comparing model performance, efficiency, and ML technique propagation between a standardized model-building approach and independent per-model optimization in recommendation systems. To facilitate this standardization, we propose the Standard Model Template (SMT) -- a framework that generates high-performance models adaptable to diverse data distributions and optimization events. By utilizing standardized, composable ML model components, SMT reduces technique propagation complexity from $O(n \cdot 2^k)$ to $O(n + k)$ where $n$ is the number of models and $k$ the number of techniques.
  Evaluating an extensive suite of models over four global development cycles within Meta's production ads ranking ecosystem, our results demonstrate: (1) a 0.63% average improvement in cross-entropy at neutral serving capacity, (2) a 92% reduction in per-model iteration engineering time, and (3) a $6.3\times$ increase in technique-model pair adoption throughput. These findings challenge the conventional wisdom that diverse optimization goals inherently require diversified ML model design.

</details>


### [33] [The Anatomy of Uncertainty in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24967)
*Aditya Taparia,Ransalu Senanayake,Kowshik Thopalli,Vivek Narayanaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将大语言模型（LLM）的不确定性分解为输入歧义、知识缺口和解码随机性三个语义组件的框架，以提供更具可操作性的模型审计与幻觉检测见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅提供单一不确定性分数或依赖经典的随机-认知二分法，无法为改进生成模型提供可操作的见解，且不足以理解LLM中的不确定性，因此需要更精细的不确定性分解框架。

Method: 提出了一个不确定性分解框架，将LLM的不确定性剖析为三个不同的语义组件：（i）源于模糊提示的输入歧义；（ii）由参数化证据不足引起的知识缺口；（iii）源于随机采样的解码随机性。

Result: 通过一系列实验证明，这三种组件的主导地位会随着模型规模和任务的变化而发生转变。该框架能更好地理解LLM的可靠性并检测幻觉。

Conclusion: 该框架为审计LLM可靠性和检测幻觉提供了更深入的理解，为针对性干预和构建更值得信赖的系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Understanding why a large language model (LLM) is uncertain about the response is important for their reliable deployment. Current approaches, which either provide a single uncertainty score or rely on the classical aleatoric-epistemic dichotomy, fail to offer actionable insights for improving the generative model. Recent studies have also shown that such methods are not enough for understanding uncertainty in LLMs. In this work, we advocate for an uncertainty decomposition framework that dissects LLM uncertainty into three distinct semantic components: (i) input ambiguity, arising from ambiguous prompts; (ii) knowledge gaps, caused by insufficient parametric evidence; and (iii) decoding randomness, stemming from stochastic sampling. Through a series of experiments we demonstrate that the dominance of these components can shift across model size and task. Our framework provides a better understanding to audit LLM reliability and detect hallucinations, paving the way for targeted interventions and more trustworthy systems.

</details>


### [34] [Rethinking Failure Attribution in Multi-Agent Systems: A Multi-Perspective Benchmark and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25001)
*Yeonjun In,Mehrab Tanjim,Jayakumar Subramanian,Sungchul Kim,Uttaran Bhattacharya,Wonjoong Kim,Sangwu Park,Somdeb Sarkhel,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.AI

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS故障归因基准和方法假设每次故障仅存在单一确定性根因，但实际多智能体系统中由于复杂的智能体间依赖关系和模糊的执行轨迹，故障往往存在多种合理的归因解释，这一现实情况被现有研究忽视。

Method: 从多视角立场重新审视MAS故障归因问题，提出多视角故障归因范式以显式处理归因模糊性；构建首个支持该范式的基准MP-Bench及配套评估协议。

Result: 通过大量实验发现，此前关于LLMs在故障归因任务上表现困难的结论主要由现有基准设计的局限性所驱动；在多视角合理设置下，LLMs表现更好。

Conclusion: 研究强调了多视角基准和评估协议对于实现现实可靠的多智能体系统调试的必要性。

Abstract: Failure attribution is essential for diagnosing and improving multi-agent systems (MAS), yet existing benchmarks and methods largely assume a single deterministic root cause for each failure. In practice, MAS failures often admit multiple plausible attributions due to complex inter-agent dependencies and ambiguous execution trajectories. We revisit MAS failure attribution from a multi-perspective standpoint and propose multi-perspective failure attribution, a practical paradigm that explicitly accounts for attribution ambiguity. To support this setting, we introduce MP-Bench, the first benchmark designed for multi-perspective failure attribution in MAS, along with a new evaluation protocol tailored to this paradigm. Through extensive experiments, we find that prior conclusions suggesting LLMs struggle with failure attribution are largely driven by limitations in existing benchmark designs. Our results highlight the necessity of multi-perspective benchmarks and evaluation protocols for realistic and reliable MAS debugging.

</details>
