Our rigorous evaluation of 11 leading models establishes a persistently low baseline, with the state-of-the-art achieving only a 19.9\% average accuracy, exposing the difficulty of formal structural stress-testing. Furthermore, our findings reveal a stark dichotomy in the current AI capabilities. While advanced deep research pipelines successfully redefine dynamic topological re-ordering and exhibit robust ontological verification -- matching pure reasoning models in falsifying hallucinated premises -- they almost universally collapse on multi-hop structural synthesis. Crucially, massive performance variance across tasks exposes a lingering reliance on brittle heuristics rather than a systemic understanding. Ultimately, this work demonstrates that while top-tier autonomous agents can now organically unify search and reasoning, achieving a generalized mastery over complex structural information remains a formidable open challenge.\footnote{Our implementation will be available at https://github.com/tzq1999/CDR.

</details>


### [108] [Hessian-informed machine learning interatomic potential towards bridging theory and experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25373)
*Bangchen Yin,Jian Ouyang,Zhen Fan,Kailai Lin,Hanshi Hu,Dingshun Lv,Weiluo Ren,Hai Xiao,Ji Chen,Changsu Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hessian指导的机器学习原子间势（Hi-MLIP），并通过高效的HINT训练协议大幅降低昂贵的Hessian标签需求，实现了对复杂体系势能面曲率的准确预测，显著改善了过渡态搜索、吉布斯自由能预测以及强非谐氢化物的处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 势能面的局部曲率对于从头预测分子和材料的实验可观测性质至关重要，但对于复杂体系，准确获取曲率信息仍然极具挑战性且计算代价高昂。

Method: 开发了名为HINT的高效训练协议，结合Hessian预训练、构型采样、课程学习和随机投影Hessian损失等关键技术，将昂贵的Hessian标签需求降低了2-4个数量级，从而使Hessian监督在实践上可行。

Result: Hi-MLIP显著改善了过渡态搜索，使吉布斯自由能预测接近化学精度（尤其在数据稀缺情况下），并能准确处理强非谐氢化物，重现声子重整化和超导临界温度，与实验结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一条切实可行的路径，通过增强机器学习原子间势的曲率感知能力，弥合了模拟与实验可观测值之间的差距，适用于广泛的体系研究。

Abstract: Local curvature of potential energy surfaces is critical for predicting certain experimental observables of molecules and materials from first principles, yet it remains far beyond reach for complex systems. In this work, we introduce a Hessian-informed Machine Learning Interatomic Potential (Hi-MLIP) that captures such curvature reliably, thereby enabling accurate analysis of associated thermodynamic and kinetic phenomena. To make Hessian supervision practically viable, we develop a highly efficient training protocol, termed Hessian INformed Training (HINT), achieving two to four orders of magnitude reduction for the requirement of expensive Hessian labels. HINT integrates critical techniques, including Hessian pre-training, configuration sampling, curriculum learning and stochastic projection Hessian loss. Enabled by HINT, Hi-MLIP significantly improves transition-state search and brings Gibbs free-energy predictions close to chemical accuracy especially in data-scarce regimes. Our framework also enables accurate treatment of strongly anharmonic hydrides, reproducing phonon renormalization and superconducting critical temperatures in close agreement with experiment while bypassing the computational bottleneck of anharmonic calculations. These results establish a practical route to enhancing curvature awareness of machine learning interatomic potentials, bridging simulation and experimental observables across a wide range of systems.

</details>


### [109] [GlowQ: Group-Shared LOw-Rank Approximation for Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25385)
*Selim An,Il hong Suh,Yeseong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GlowQ，一种面向量化大语言模型的分组共享低秩近似方法，通过缓存共享因子并选择性恢复关键层，有效解决了现有低秩校正方法延迟和内存开销大的问题，显著提升了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化技术（如4-bit量化）虽然广泛应用，但会导致精度下降。低秩校正方法（如LQER、QERA、ASER）虽然能缓解此问题，但需要对所有层进行恢复并在每个解码器块中插入误差校正模块，从而增加了延迟和内存开销。

Method: 提出GlowQ方法，为每个输入共享组缓存一个单一的共享右因子，仅对精度收益最高的组或层进行恢复。该方法在每个输入共享组计算一次高精度投影，并跨模块复用，减少了参数和内存开销。此外，还提出了选择性变体GlowQ-S，仅在收益最大的地方应用缓存的共享模块。

Result: 与强基线相比，GlowQ平均减少TTFB 5.6%，提升吞吐量9.6%，WikiText-2困惑度降低0.17%，下游任务精度提升0.42个百分点。选择性模型GlowQ-S进一步减少延迟，TTFB降低23.4%，吞吐量提升37.4%，平均精度保持在0.2个百分点以内。

Conclusion: GlowQ成功解决了量化大语言模型中精度与效率的权衡问题，通过分组共享和选择性恢复策略，在保持模型精度的同时显著降低了推理延迟和内存开销，为大语言模型的部署提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantization techniques such as BitsAndBytes, AWQ, and GPTQ are widely used as a standard method in deploying large language models but often degrades accuracy when using low-bit representations, e.g., 4 bits. Low-rank correction methods (e.g., LQER, QERA, ASER) has been proposed to mitigate this issue, however, they restore all layers and insert error-correction modules into every decoder block, which increases latency and memory overhead. To address this limitation, we propose GlowQ, a group-shared low-rank approximation for quantized LLMs that caches a single shared right factor per input-sharing group and restores only the groups or layers that yield the highest accuracy benefit. GlowQ computes the high-precision projection once per input-sharing group and reuses it across its modules, reducing parameter and memory overhead, and retaining the expressivity of layer-specific corrections. We also propose a selective variant, GlowQ-S, that applies the cached shared module only where it provides the largest benefit. Compared with strong baselines, our approach reduces TTFB by (5.6%) and increases throughput by (9.6%) on average, while reducing perplexity on WikiText-2 by (0.17%) and increasing downstream accuracy by 0.42 percentage points. The selective model GlowQ-S further reduces latency, cutting TTFB by (23.4%) and increasing throughput by (37.4%), while maintaining accuracy within 0.2 percentage points on average.

</details>


### [110] [Not a fragment, but the whole: Map-based evaluation of data-driven Fire Danger Index models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25469)
*Shahbaz Alvi,Italo Epicoco,Jose Maria Costa Saura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向实际决策的林火预测模型评估新方法，系统分析了预测准确性与误报率，并证明集成学习能同时提升火情识别能力并降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的野火预测研究多采用标准分类指标进行评估，忽略了误报率在实际林火预报业务中的关键影响，导致评估结果难以真实反映模型的业务性能。

Method: 重新审视日尺度火险指数（FDI）模型评估范式，提出一种与实际决策过程对齐的评估方法，系统性地评估模型在预测火情活动准确性及误报（假阳性）方面的表现。

Result: 实验表明，机器学习集成模型不仅提升了火情识别的准确性，还有效降低了误报率，展现出更优的综合预测性能。

Conclusion: 面向实际业务场景的评估方法能更全面地衡量模型价值，集成学习策略为提高林火预测精度与降低误报提供了有效途径。

Abstract: A growing body of literature has focused on predicting wildfire occurrence using machine learning methods, capitalizing on high-resolution data and fire predictors that canonical process-based frameworks largely ignore. Standard evaluation metrics for an ML classifier, while important, provide a potentially limited measure of the model's operational performance for the Fire Danger Index (FDI) forecast. Furthermore, model evaluation is frequently conducted without adequately accounting for false positive rates, despite their critical relevance in operational contexts. In this paper, we revisit the daily FDI model evaluation paradigm and propose a novel method for evaluating a forest fire forecasting model that is aligned with real-world decision-making. Furthermore, we systematically assess performance in accurately predicting fire activity and the false positives (false alarms). We further demonstrate that an ensemble of ML models improves both fire identification and reduces false positives.

</details>


### [111] [Causal-INSIGHT: Probing Temporal Models to Extract Causal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25473)
*Benjamin Redden,Hui Wang,Shuyan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Causal-INSIGHT，一个模型无关、事后解释框架，用于从训练好的时间预测器中提取有向时滞影响结构，并引入Qbic稀疏感知图选择准则，在时滞定位方面取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多变量时间序列中的有向时序相互作用对于解释复杂动态系统和在其上训练的预测模型至关重要。现有方法通常在数据生成过程层面推断因果结构，但缺乏针对预训练预测器所依赖的影响结构进行分析的工具。

Method: 提出Causal-INSIGHT框架，通过在推理时应用受干预启发的输入钳制（input clamping），分析固定的预训练预测器对这些系统性干预的响应，构建反映预测器依赖关系的有向时间影响信号；引入Qbic稀疏感知图选择准则，在无需真实图标签的情况下平衡预测保真度和结构复杂度。

Result: 在合成、仿真和现实基准测试上的实验表明，Causal-INSIGHT能够跨多种骨干架构泛化，保持竞争性的结构准确性，并在应用于现有预测器时显著改善时滞定位性能。

Conclusion: Causal-INSIGHT为理解预训练时间预测器的依赖结构提供了一种有效的模型无关方法，能够准确提取模型隐含的有向时滞影响关系，为复杂动态系统的解释性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Understanding directed temporal interactions in multivariate time series is essential for interpreting complex dynamical systems and the predictive models trained on them. We present Causal-INSIGHT, a model-agnostic, post-hoc interpretation framework for extracting model-implied (predictor-dependent), directed, time-lagged influence structure from trained temporal predictors. Rather than inferring causal structure at the level of the data-generating process, Causal-INSIGHT analyzes how a fixed, pre-trained predictor responds to systematic, intervention-inspired input clamping applied at inference time.
  From these responses, we construct directed temporal influence signals that reflect the dependencies the predictor relies on for prediction, and introduce Qbic, a sparsity-aware graph selection criterion that balances predictive fidelity and structural complexity without requiring ground-truth graph labels. Experiments across synthetic, simulated, and realistic benchmarks show that Causal-INSIGHT generalizes across diverse backbone architectures, maintains competitive structural accuracy, and yields significant improvements in temporal delay localization when applied to existing predictors.

</details>


### [112] [How Class Ontology and Data Scale Affect Audio Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25476)
*Manuel Milling,Andreas Triantafyllopoulos,Alexander Gebhard,Simon Rampp,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究表明，在音频迁移学习中，预训练数据规模的增长虽有助益，但预训练任务与下游任务之间的相似性才是决定迁移效果的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管迁移学习在深度学习中应用广泛，但其内部工作机制仍有诸多未解之谜，特别是关于何时以及为何迁移学习效果良好的问题尚缺乏深入理解。

Method: 研究聚焦音频到音频的迁移学习，在AudioSet的（基于本体论的）子集上预训练多种模型状态，并在声学场景识别、鸟类活动识别和语音命令识别三个计算机听觉任务上进行微调。

Result: 实验结果表明，增加预训练数据中的样本数量和类别数量均对迁移学习产生积极影响；然而，预训练任务与下游任务之间的相似性通常更为重要，这种相似性能够引导模型学习到可比较的特征。

Conclusion: 迁移学习的有效性不仅取决于预训练数据的规模，更关键的是预训练任务与目标任务之间的相关性，任务相似性能够带来更优的特征迁移效果。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a crucial concept within deep learning that allows artificial neural networks to benefit from a large pre-training data basis when confronted with a task of limited data. Despite its ubiquitous use and clear benefits, there are still many open questions regarding the inner workings of transfer learning and, in particular, regarding the understanding of when and how well it works. To that extent, we perform a rigorous study focusing on audio-to-audio transfer learning, in which we pre-train various model states on (ontology-based) subsets of AudioSet and fine-tune them on three computer audition tasks, namely acoustic scene recognition, bird activity recognition, and speech command recognition. We report that increasing the number of samples and classes in the pre-training data both have a positive impact on transfer learning. This is, however, generally surpassed by similarity between pre-training and the downstream task, which can lead the model to learn comparable features.

</details>


### [113] [Interpretable PM2.5 Forecasting for Urban Air Quality: A Comparative Study of Operational Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25495)
*Moazzam Umer Gondal,Hamad ul Qudous,Asma Ahmad Farhan,Sultan Alamri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对北京PM2.5小时级预测，证明轻量级可解释模型（SARIMAX和Facebook Prophet）在合理的工作流设计下，能够以更低的计算成本达到与复杂模型相当的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前许多短期空气质量预测框架依赖复杂、数据密集且计算需求高的模型，本研究旨在探究轻量级、可解释的预测方法是否能为北京PM2.5小时级预测提供具有竞争力的性能。

Method: 基于多年污染物和气象时间序列数据，构建了包含按时间顺序的数据划分、预处理、特征选择和外生驱动因子建模的防泄露预测工作流（Perfect Prognosis设置）。评估了SARIMAX、Facebook Prophet和NeuralProphet三种预测模型，并在两种自适应机制下测试：每周滚动向前重新拟合和冻结模型结合在线残差修正。

Result: 在滚动向前重新拟合机制下，Facebook Prophet表现最佳（MAE 37.61，RMSE 50.10），计算时间显著低于NeuralProphet。在冻结模型机制下，在线残差修正提升了SARIMAX和Facebook Prophet的性能，修正后的SARIMAX达到最低误差（MAE 32.50，RMSE 46.85）。NeuralProphet在两种机制下准确性较低且不稳定。修正后的Facebook Prophet在将运行时间从15分21.91秒降至46.60秒的同时，达到了与其滚动向前重新拟合版本几乎相同的误差水平。

Conclusion: 轻量级加性预测策略在城市空气质量预测中具有高度竞争力，能够在预测准确性、可解释性和计算效率之间提供实用的平衡。

Abstract: Accurate short-term air-quality forecasting is essential for public health protection and urban management, yet many recent forecasting frameworks rely on complex, data-intensive, and computationally demanding models. This study investigates whether lightweight and interpretable forecasting approaches can provide competitive performance for hourly PM2.5 prediction in Beijing, China. Using multi-year pollutant and meteorological time-series data, we developed a leakage-aware forecasting workflow that combined chronological data partitioning, preprocessing, feature selection, and exogenous-driver modeling under the Perfect Prognosis setting. Three forecasting families were evaluated: SARIMAX, Facebook Prophet, and NeuralProphet. To assess practical deployment behavior, the models were tested under two adaptive regimes: weekly walk-forward refitting and frozen forecasting with online residual correction. Results showed clear differences in both predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. Under walk-forward refitting, Facebook Prophet achieved the strongest completed performance, with an MAE of $37.61$ and an RMSE of $50.10$, while also requiring substantially less execution time than NeuralProphet. In the frozen-model regime, online residual correction improved Facebook Prophet and SARIMAX, with corrected SARIMAX yielding the lowest overall error (MAE $32.50$; RMSE $46.85$). NeuralProphet remained less accurate and less stable across both regimes, and residual correction did not improve its forecasts. Notably, corrected Facebook Prophet reached nearly the same error as its walk-forward counterpart while reducing runtime from $15$ min $21.91$ sec to $46.60$ sec. These findings show that lightweight additive forecasting strategies can remain highly competitive for urban air-quality prediction, offering a practical balance between accuracy, interpretability, ...

</details>


### [114] [Missing-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Unified Microservice Incident Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25538)
*Wenzhuo Qian,Hailiang Zhao,Ziqi Wang,Zhipeng Gao,Jiayi Chen,Zhiwei Ling,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARMOR，一个面向缺失模态场景的自监督框架，通过模态特定非对称编码器和缺失感知门控融合机制，在数据不完整的情况下实现了鲁棒的微服务事件管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的微服务事件管理统一框架假设数据完整性，但实际中网络波动和代理故障常导致模态缺失，而基于静态占位符的方法会引入填充噪声，掩盖异常并降低性能。

Method: 提出ARMOR框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 模态特定非对称编码器，隔离指标、日志和追踪之间的分布差异；(2) 缺失感知门控融合机制，利用可学习占位符和动态偏差补偿防止不完整输入的跨模态干扰。采用带掩码引导重建的自监督自回归方法联合优化异常检测(AD)、故障分诊(FT)和根因定位(RCL)。

Result: 实验表明ARMOR在完整数据条件下达到最先进性能，即使在严重模态缺失情况下仍保持鲁棒的诊断准确性。

Conclusion: ARMOR成功解决了微服务事件管理中的模态缺失问题，在完整和不完整数据场景下均表现出色，为实际应用提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated incident management is critical for microservice reliability. While recent unified frameworks leverage multimodal data for joint optimization, they unrealistically assume perfect data completeness. In practice, network fluctuations and agent failures frequently cause missing modalities. Existing approaches relying on static placeholders introduce imputation noise that masks anomalies and degrades performance. To address this, we propose ARMOR, a robust self-supervised framework designed for missing modality scenarios. ARMOR features: (i) a modality-specific asymmetric encoder that isolates distribution disparities among metrics, logs, and traces; and (ii) a missing-aware gated fusion mechanism utilizing learnable placeholders and dynamic bias compensation to prevent cross-modal interference from incomplete inputs. By employing self-supervised auto-regression with mask-guided reconstruction, ARMOR jointly optimizes anomaly detection (AD), failure triage (FT), and root cause localization (RCL). AD and RCL require no fault labels, while FT relies solely on failure-type annotations for the downstream classifier. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ARMOR achieves state-of-the-art performance under complete data conditions and maintains robust diagnostic accuracy even with severe modality loss.

</details>


### [115] [Revisiting On-Policy Distillation: Empirical Failure Modes and Simple Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25562)
*Yuqian Fu,Haohuan Huang,Kaiwen Jiang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了同策略蒸馏（OPD）在长序列大语言模型训练中的脆弱性，提出了带有teacher top-K局部支持匹配的截断反向KL目标，解决了采样token OPD的三个失效模式，在数学推理和多任务训练中取得了更稳定的优化和更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）在大语言模型后训练中具有吸引力，因为它在学生生成的轨迹上评估教师反馈。然而在长序列设置中，常见的采样token变体很脆弱：它将分布匹配简化为单token信号，并且随着轨迹偏离教师常见的prefix而变得越来越不可靠。

Method: 从估计器和实现两个角度重新审视OPD。理论分析token级OPD相对于序列级反向KL存在偏差，但具有更紧的最坏情况方差界限。提出了teacher top-K局部支持匹配方法，实现为截断反向KL配合top-p rollout采样和特殊token掩码，以解决不平衡的单token信号、学生生成prefix上不可靠的教师引导、以及tokenizer或特殊token不匹配造成的扭曲等问题。

Result: 实验识别了采样token OPD的三个失效模式：不平衡的单token信号、学生生成prefix上不可靠的教师引导、以及tokenizer或特殊token不匹配造成的扭曲。所提出的方法在单任务数学推理和多任务智能体加数学训练中，比采样token OPD产生更稳定的优化和更好的下游性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的带有teacher top-K局部支持匹配的截断反向KL目标有效解决了采样token OPD的关键问题，为长序列大语言模型训练提供了更稳定和有效的蒸馏方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is appealing for large language model (LLM) post-training because it evaluates teacher feedback on student-generated rollouts rather than fixed teacher traces. In long-horizon settings, however, the common sampled-token variant is fragile: it reduces distribution matching to a one-token signal and becomes increasingly unreliable as rollouts drift away from prefixes the teacher commonly visits. We revisit OPD from the estimator and implementation sides. Theoretically, token-level OPD is biased relative to sequence-level reverse-KL, but it has a much tighter worst-case variance bound; our toy study shows the same tradeoff empirically, with stronger future-reward coupling producing higher gradient variance and less stable learning. Empirically, we identify three failure modes of sampled-token OPD: an imbalanced one-token signal, unreliable teacher guidance on student-generated prefixes, and distortions caused by tokenizer or special-token mismatch. We address these issues with teacher top-K local support matching, implemented as truncated reverse-KL with top-p rollout sampling and special-token masking. Across single-task math reasoning and multi-task agentic-plus-math training, this objective yields more stable optimization and better downstream performance than sampled-token OPD.

</details>


### [116] [Spatiotemporal System Forecasting with Irregular Time Steps via Masked Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25597)
*Kewei Zhu,Yanze Xin,Jinwei Hu,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Yiming Yang,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理时空掩码自编码器，解决非规则时间步长的高维动力系统预测问题，在预测精度、鲁棒性和计算效率方面显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据驱动算法在处理具有非规则时间步长的高维动力系统预测时面临重大挑战。这些不规则性源于数据缺失、稀疏观测或自适应计算技术，会降低预测精度。

Method: 提出物理时空掩码自编码器，将卷积自编码器用于空间特征提取，与针对非规则时间序列优化的掩码自编码器相结合，利用注意力机制在单次预测过程中重建整个物理序列，无需数据填补即可保持系统的物理完整性。

Result: 在多个模拟数据集和真实海洋温度数据上的评估表明，该方法在预测精度、对非线性的鲁棒性和计算效率方面均显著优于传统卷积和循环网络方法。

Conclusion: 该模型无需领域特定知识即可捕捉复杂的时空模式，在气候建模、流体动力学、海洋预报、环境监测和科学计算领域具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Predicting high-dimensional dynamical systems with irregular time steps presents significant challenges for current data-driven algorithms. These irregularities arise from missing data, sparse observations, or adaptive computational techniques, reducing prediction accuracy. To address these limitations, we propose a novel method: a Physics-Spatiotemporal Masked Autoencoder. This method integrates convolutional autoencoders for spatial feature extraction with masked autoencoders optimised for irregular time series, leveraging attention mechanisms to reconstruct the entire physical sequence in a single prediction pass. The model avoids the need for data imputation while preserving physical integrity of the system. Here, 'physics' refers to high-dimensional fields generated by underlying dynamical systems, rather than the enforcement of explicit physical constraints or PDE residuals. We evaluate this approach on multiple simulated datasets and real-world ocean temperature data. The results demonstrate that our method achieves significant improvements in prediction accuracy, robustness to nonlinearities, and computational efficiency over traditional convolutional and recurrent network methods. The model shows potential for capturing complex spatiotemporal patterns without requiring domain-specific knowledge, with applications in climate modelling, fluid dynamics, ocean forecasting, environmental monitoring, and scientific computing.

</details>


### [117] [Social Hippocampus Memory Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25614)
*Liping Yi,Zhiming Zhao,Qinghua Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SoHip框架，一种基于记忆共享而非模型共享的联邦学习方法，用于解决异构智能体之间的协作学习问题，在保证隐私的同时提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异构联邦学习方法通常通过共享模型参数或中间表示进行协作，这可能导致敏感信息泄露并产生额外开销。作者受社会学习理论启发，旨在通过共享抽象知识（记忆）而非模型本身来实现安全高效的协作学习。

Method: 提出SoHip（Social Hippocampus Memory Learning）框架。该方法受海马体机制启发，将智能体的局部表示抽象为短期记忆，通过巩固机制转化为长期记忆，并将其与全局聚合的长期记忆融合以增强预测。过程中原始数据和本地模型保留在本地，仅交换轻量级记忆，并提供了收敛性和隐私保护的理论分析。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上与七个基线方法进行对比实验。结果表明，SoHip始终优于现有方法，准确率提升最高达8.78%。理论分析验证了算法的收敛性与隐私保护特性。

Conclusion: SoHip成功实现了一种以记忆为中心的社会化机器学习范式，有效解决了异构联邦学习中的隐私泄露与通信开销问题，在保护数据隐私的前提下显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Social learning highlights that learning agents improve not in isolation, but through interaction and structured knowledge exchange with others. When introduced into machine learning, this principle gives rise to social machine learning (SML), where multiple agents collaboratively learn by sharing abstracted knowledge. Federated learning (FL) provides a natural collaboration substrate for this paradigm, yet existing heterogeneous FL approaches often rely on sharing model parameters or intermediate representations, which may expose sensitive information and incur additional overhead. In this work, we propose SoHip (Social Hippocampus Memory Learning), a memory-centric social machine learning framework that enables collaboration among heterogeneous agents via memory sharing rather than model sharing. SoHip abstracts each agent's individual short-term memory from local representations, consolidates it into individual long-term memory through a hippocampus-inspired mechanism, and fuses it with collectively aggregated long-term memory to enhance local prediction. Throughout the process, raw data and local models remain on-device, while only lightweight memory are exchanged. We provide theoretical analysis on convergence and privacy preservation properties. Experiments on two benchmark datasets with seven baselines demonstrate that SoHip consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving up to 8.78% accuracy improvements.

</details>


### [118] [Anchored-Branched Steady-state WInd Flow Transformer (AB-SWIFT): a metamodel for 3D atmospheric flow in urban environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25635)
*Armand de Villeroché,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Vincent Le Guen,Sibo Cheng,Marc Bocquet,Alban Farchi,Patrick Armand,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AB-SWIFT，一种基于Transformer的深度学习代理模型，用于替代昂贵的CFD计算进行局部尺度气流建模，在随机城市几何和不同大气分层条件下取得了最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部尺度气流建模对污染物扩散和风电场建模等应用至关重要，但传统CFD计算成本高昂。现有深度学习代理模型难以适应城市几何的高度变化和大规模网格尺寸。

Method: 引入AB-SWIFT（Anchored Branched Steady-state WInd Flow Transformer），这是一种具有内部分支结构的Transformer模型，专为大气流动建模而设计。模型在专门构建的随机城市几何大气模拟数据库上进行训练，涵盖不稳定、中性和稳定大气分层条件。

Result: 与最先进的Transformer和图神经网络模型相比，AB-SWIFT在所有预测场上均达到了最高精度。

Conclusion: AB-SWIFT成功解决了深度学习模型在城市气流建模中的适应性挑战，为局部尺度气流建模提供了一种高效准确的替代方案，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Air flow modeling at a local scale is essential for applications such as pollutant dispersion modeling or wind farm modeling. To circumvent costly Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) computations, deep learning surrogate models have recently emerged as promising alternatives. However, in the context of urban air flow, deep learning models struggle to adapt to the high variations of the urban geometry and to large mesh sizes. To tackle these challenges, we introduce Anchored Branched Steady-state WInd Flow Transformer (AB-SWIFT), a transformer-based model with an internal branched structure uniquely designed for atmospheric flow modeling. We train our model on a specially designed database of atmospheric simulations around randomised urban geometries and with a mixture of unstable, neutral, and stable atmospheric stratifications. Our model reaches the best accuracy on all predicted fields compared to state-of-the-art transformers and graph-based models. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/abswift.

</details>


### [119] [Uncertainty-Guided Label Rebalancing for CPS Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25670)
*John Ayotunde,Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Lionel C. Briand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出U-Balance方法，利用行为不确定性指导的标签重平衡机制(uLNR)解决CPS安全监测中的极端类别不平衡问题，在UAV基准数据集上F1分数达到0.806，超越最强基线14.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络物理系统(CPS)安全监测中不安全事件极为罕见，造成极端类别不平衡（46:1），导致安全预测器性能下降。标准重平衡技术在时序CPS遥测数据上表现不佳，要么生成不真实的合成样本，要么在少数类上过拟合。同时，CPS操作中的行为不确定性与安全结果相关但尚未在安全监测中被探索利用。

Method: U-Balance是一种监督学习方法：首先训练基于GatedMLP的不确定性预测器，将遥测窗口总结为分布运动学特征并输出不确定性分数；然后应用不确定性引导的标签重平衡(uLNR)机制，以概率方式将具有异常高不确定性但标记为安全的窗口重新标记为不安全，从而在不合成新数据的情况下用信息丰富的边界样本丰富少数类；最后在重平衡数据集上训练安全预测器。

Result: 在大规模UAV基准数据集（46:1安全/不安全比例）上验证：确认行为不确定性与安全性之间存在中等但显著的相关性；uLNR被识别为利用不确定性信息的最有效策略（优于直接早融合和晚融合）；U-Balance实现0.806的F1分数，超越最强基线14.3个百分点，同时保持具有竞争力的推理效率。消融研究证实GatedMLP不确定性预测器和uLNR机制均对效果有显著贡献。

Conclusion: U-Balance通过创新性地利用行为不确定性进行标签重平衡，有效解决了CPS安全监测中的极端类别不平衡问题，显著提升了安全预测性能，为CPS安全监测提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: Safety monitoring is essential for Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). However, unsafe events are rare in real-world CPS operations, creating an extreme class imbalance that degrades safety predictors. Standard rebalancing techniques perform poorly on time-series CPS telemetry, either generating unrealistic synthetic samples or overfitting on the minority class. Meanwhile, behavioral uncertainty in CPS operations, defined as the degree of doubt or uncertainty in CPS decisions , is often correlated with safety outcomes but unexplored in safety monitoring. To that end, we propose U-Balance, a supervised approach that leverages behavioral uncertainty to rebalance imbalanced datasets prior to training a safety predictor. U-Balance first trains a GatedMLP-based uncertainty predictor that summarizes each telemetry window into distributional kinematic features and outputs an uncertainty score. It then applies an uncertainty-guided label rebalancing (uLNR) mechanism that probabilistically relabels \textit{safe}-labeled windows with unusually high uncertainty as \textit{unsafe}, thereby enriching the minority class with informative boundary samples without synthesizing new data. Finally, a safety predictor is trained on the rebalanced dataset for safety monitoring. We evaluate U-Balance on a large-scale UAV benchmark with a 46:1 safe-to-unsafe ratio. Results confirm a moderate but significant correlation between behavioral uncertainty and safety. We then identify uLNR as the most effective strategy to exploit uncertainty information, compared to direct early and late fusion. U-Balance achieves a 0.806 F1 score, outperforming the strongest baseline by 14.3 percentage points, while maintaining competitive inference efficiency. Ablation studies confirm that both the GatedMLP-based uncertainty predictor and the uLNR mechanism contribute significantly to U-Balance's effectiveness.

</details>


### [120] [Longitudinal Digital Phenotyping for Early Cognitive-Motor Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25673)
*Diego Jimenez-Oviedo,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Ruben Tolosana,Juan Carlos Ruiz-Garcia,Jaime Herreros-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于AI的纵向框架，利用平板电脑触屏交互数据识别出三种不同的儿童认知运动发育表型（低、中、高表现组），发现低表现组具有高稳定性（>90%保留率），验证了数字生物标志物用于早期发育筛查的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的认知运动发育评估方法严重依赖主观、静态的评价，难以实现早期及时发现非典型发育。为了解决这一问题，研究旨在利用数字设备进行连续、客观的监测，通过数字生物标志器追踪儿童的发育轨迹，以实现及时干预。

Method: 研究收集了18个月至8岁儿童在多个学年内使用平板电脑完成六项认知运动任务（如精细运动控制、反应时间）的数据。采用t-SNE进行降维处理，并应用K-Means++无监督聚类算法识别不同的发育表型，同时纵向追踪个体在这些表型间的转换情况。

Result: 分析揭示了三种明显的表现特征：低、中、高表现组。纵向追踪显示低表现组具有极高的稳定性（在早期保留率超过90%），表明若无干预早期缺陷倾向于持续存在；相反，高表现组表现出更大的变异性。研究验证了在触屏数据上应用无监督学习能够揭示异质性的发育路径。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了利用触屏数据和无监督学习方法发现发育表型的有效性，这些表型可作为认知成长的可扩展、数据驱动的代理指标，为早期筛查工具和个性化儿科干预措施的制定奠定了基础。

Abstract: Early detection of atypical cognitive-motor development is critical for timely intervention, yet traditional assessments rely heavily on subjective, static evaluations. The integration of digital devices offers an opportunity for continuous, objective monitoring through digital biomarkers. In this work, we propose an AI-driven longitudinal framework to model developmental trajectories in children aged 18 months to 8 years. Using a dataset of tablet-based interactions collected over multiple academic years, we analyzed six cognitive-motor tasks (e.g., fine motor control, reaction time). We applied dimensionality reduction (t-SNE) and unsupervised clustering (K-Means++) to identify distinct developmental phenotypes and tracked individual transitions between these profiles over time. Our analysis reveals three distinct profiles: low, medium, and high performance. Crucially, longitudinal tracking highlights a high stability in the low-performance cluster (>90% retention in early years), suggesting that early deficits tend to persist without intervention. Conversely, higher-performance clusters show greater variability, potentially reflecting engagement factors. This study validates the use of unsupervised learning on touchscreen data to uncover heterogeneous developmental paths. The identified profiles serve as scalable, data-driven proxies for cognitive growth, offering a foundation for early screening tools and personalized pediatric interventions.

</details>


### [121] [On Neural Scaling Laws for Weather Emulation through Continual Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25687)
*Shashank Subramanian,Alexander Kiefer,Arnur Nigmetov,Amir Gholami,Dmitriy Morozov,Michael W. Mahoney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在科学机器学习（天气预报）领域验证了神经缩放定律的有效性，通过极简架构和周期性冷却的训练策略实现了可预测的性能扩展，并确定了计算最优训练机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律在自然语言处理和计算机视觉领域已成为构建基础模型的基石，但在科学机器学习领域的应用尚未充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，专注于天气预报模型，研究该领域中的神经缩放行为。

Method: 采用最小化、可扩展的通用Swin Transformer架构，配合恒定学习率和周期性冷却的持续训练策略。通过系统性地探索不同模型规模、数据集大小和计算预算，构建了IsoFLOP曲线。同时重新利用冷却阶段来改善下游性能（如多步滚动预测和通过频谱损失调整获得更清晰的预测）。

Result: 研究表明，采用这种极简方式训练的模型遵循可预测的缩放趋势，甚至优于标准的余弦学习率调度。成功识别了计算最优训练机制，并通过将趋势外推到更大规模揭示了潜在的性能极限，证明神经缩放可以作为高效资源分配的重要诊断工具。

Conclusion: 神经缩放定律在科学机器学习领域同样适用且具有重要价值，可作为高效资源分配的诊断工具。本文的研究方法和发现为该领域的模型扩展提供了重要参考，作者开源了代码以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws, which in some domains can predict the performance of large neural networks as a function of model, data, and compute scale, are the cornerstone of building foundation models in Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision. We study neural scaling in Scientific Machine Learning, focusing on models for weather forecasting. To analyze scaling behavior in as simple a setting as possible, we adopt a minimal, scalable, general-purpose Swin Transformer architecture, and we use continual training with constant learning rates and periodic cooldowns as an efficient training strategy. We show that models trained in this minimalist way follow predictable scaling trends and even outperform standard cosine learning rate schedules. Cooldown phases can be re-purposed to improve downstream performance, e.g., enabling accurate multi-step rollouts over longer forecast horizons as well as sharper predictions through spectral loss adjustments. We also systematically explore a wide range of model and dataset sizes under various compute budgets to construct IsoFLOP curves, and we identify compute-optimal training regimes. Extrapolating these trends to larger scales highlights potential performance limits, demonstrating that neural scaling can serve as an important diagnostic for efficient resource allocation. We open-source our code for reproducibility.

</details>


### [122] [Neural Network Conversion of Machine Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25699)
*Man-Ling Sung,Jan Silovsky,Man-Hung Siu,Herbert Gish,Chinnu Pittapally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的知识蒸馏方法，将非神经网络的机器学习模型（随机森林）作为教师模型，迁移到神经网络学生模型，实现了对传统ML流程的联合优化和统一推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识蒸馏方法主要关注从大型神经网络教师模型向小型神经网络学生模型的迁移，但许多实际应用场景中表现优异的模型（如随机森林）并非神经网络结构。作者希望将这些非神经网络模型的知识迁移到神经网络中，以实现：（1）对传统机器学习流程各组件的联合优化；（2）构建一个能处理多种ML任务的统一神经网络推理引擎。

Method: 提出将随机森林分类器作为教师模型，通过知识蒸馏技术将知识迁移到神经网络学生模型。研究者在100个OpenML任务上进行实验（这些任务中随机森林表现优异），测试了多种神经网络拓扑结构，并探讨了如何选择合适的神经网络超参数。

Result: 实验结果表明，在大多数OpenML任务中，只要选择合适的神经网络超参数，神经网络学生模型确实能够成功模拟随机森林教师模型的性能。研究者还验证了可以利用随机森林本身来辅助选择神经网络的最佳超参数。

Conclusion: 从非神经网络机器学习模型向神经网络进行知识蒸馏是可行的，这为将传统ML流程统一到神经网络框架提供了一条有效途径，同时保留了原有模型的高性能优势。

Abstract: Transfer learning and knowledge distillation has recently gained a lot of attention in the deep learning community. One transfer approach, the student-teacher learning, has been shown to successfully create ``small'' student neural networks that mimic the performance of a much bigger and more complex ``teacher'' networks. In this paper, we investigate an extension to this approach and transfer from a non-neural-based machine learning pipeline as teacher to a neural network (NN) student, which would allow for joint optimization of the various pipeline components and a single unified inference engine for multiple ML tasks. In particular, we explore replacing the random forest classifier by transfer learning to a student NN. We experimented with various NN topologies on 100 OpenML tasks in which random forest has been one of the best solutions. Our results show that for the majority of the tasks, the student NN can indeed mimic the teacher if one can select the right NN hyper-parameters. We also investigated the use of random forest for selecting the right NN hyper-parameters.

</details>


### [123] [Maximum Entropy Behavior Exploration for Sim2Real Zero-Shot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25464)
*Jiajun Hu,Nuria Armengol Urpi,Jin Cheng,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FB-MEBE算法，一种面向四足机器人控制的在线零样本强化学习方法。通过结合无监督行为探索策略和正则化评论家，解决了无向探索导致的数据多样性低和策略不自然的问题，实现了在仿真和真实硬件上的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本强化学习算法在预训练数据集的收集上存在挑战：无向探索策略产生的数据多样性低，导致下游任务性能不佳，且生成的策略难以直接部署到真实机器人硬件上。研究者旨在解决在线零样本RL中数据质量和策略可部署性的问题。

Method: 提出了FB-MEBE算法，建立在Forward-Backward (FB)算法基础之上。该方法包含两个核心组件：(1)无监督行为探索策略，通过最大化达成行为分布的熵来促进探索，增加数据多样性；(2)正则化评论家，用于塑造恢复的策略，使其更加自然和物理上合理。该算法专门针对四足机器人控制系统设计。

Result: 在多个模拟下游任务中，FB-MEBE相比其他探索策略实现了性能提升。更重要的是，该算法生成的策略具有自然性，可以无缝部署到真实硬件平台，无需额外的微调过程，证明了其在实际机器人控制中的实用性。

Conclusion: FB-MEBE成功地将在线零样本强化学习应用于真实的四足机器人控制系统。通过引入基于熵最大化的探索策略和正则化机制，该方法既提升了下游任务性能，又确保了策略的物理合理性和硬件可部署性，为零样本RL在机器人领域的实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms aim to learn a family of policies from a reward-free dataset, and recover optimal policies for any reward function directly at test time. Naturally, the quality of the pretraining dataset determines the performance of the recovered policies across tasks. However, pre-collecting a relevant, diverse dataset without prior knowledge of the downstream tasks of interest remains a challenge. In this work, we study $\textit{online}$ zero-shot RL for quadrupedal control on real robotic systems, building upon the Forward-Backward (FB) algorithm. We observe that undirected exploration yields low-diversity data, leading to poor downstream performance and rendering policies impractical for direct hardware deployment. Therefore, we introduce FB-MEBE, an online zero-shot RL algorithm that combines an unsupervised behavior exploration strategy with a regularization critic. FB-MEBE promotes exploration by maximizing the entropy of the achieved behavior distribution. Additionally, a regularization critic shapes the recovered policies toward more natural and physically plausible behaviors. We empirically demonstrate that FB-MEBE achieves and improved performance compared to other exploration strategies in a range of simulated downstream tasks, and that it renders natural policies that can be seamlessly deployed to hardware without further finetuning. Videos and code available on our website.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Scalable Air-to-Ground Wireless Channel Modeling Using Environmental Context and Generative Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24620)
*Jingyi Tian,Lin Cai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种环境感知的空地无线信道建模方法，结合真实环境数据与射线追踪技术，并利用扩散模型实现高效预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道（LEO）卫星的高速运动导致传播信道快速变化，且其行为强烈受周围环境影响，尤其在低仰角时信号极易受地形阻挡和其他环境效应影响。现有研究多依赖假设的统计信道分布，忽略了真实地理环境的影响。

Method: 利用数字高程模型（DEMs）、土地覆盖信息等真实环境数据，结合射线追踪（RT）判断链路是视距（LOS）还是非视距（NLOS），并识别信号的反射路径。将阻挡和反射剖面与衍射损耗、植被吸收和大气衰减模型相结合，定量刻画信道行为。为解决RT计算量大问题，使用RT生成的样本和环境特征训练可扩展的扩散模型，以高效预测任意卫星与地面终端位置的信道性能。

Result: 通过蜂窝和LEO卫星链路的实测数据验证了所提模型在真实环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够高效、准确地建模和预测复杂地理环境下的LEO卫星信道性能，支持实时决策。

Abstract: The fast motion of Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellites causes the propagation channel to vary rapidly, and its behavior is strongly shaped by the surrounding environment, especially at low elevation angles where signals are highly susceptible to terrain blockage and other environmental effects. Existing studies mostly rely on assumed statistical channel distributions and therefore ignore the influence of the actual geographic environment. In this paper, we propose an environment-aware channel modeling method for air-to-ground wireless links. We leverage real environmental data, including digital elevation models (DEMs) and land cover information, together with ray tracing (RT) to determine whether a link is line-of-sight (LOS) or non-line-of-sight (NLOS) and to identify possible reflection paths of the signal. The resulting obstruction and reflection profiles are then combined with models of diffraction loss, vegetation absorption, and atmospheric attenuation to quantitatively characterize channel behavior in realistic geographic environments. Since RT is computationally intensive, we use RT-generated samples and environmental features to train a scalable diffusion model that can efficiently predict channel performance for arbitrary satellite and ground terminal positions, thereby supporting real-time decision-making. In the experiments, we validate the proposed model with measurement data from both cellular and LEO satellite links, demonstrating its effectiveness in realistic environments.

</details>


### [125] [Dual-Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Handover Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24634)
*Matteo Salvatori,Filippo Vannella,Sebastian Macaluso,Stylianos E. Trevlakis,Carlos Segura Perales,José Suarez-Varela,Alexandros-Apostolos A. Boulogeorgos,Ioannis Arapakis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双重图上分散式多智能体强化学习（TD3-D-MA）的方法，通过优化切换控制参数（CIO）来提升蜂窝网络中的切换性能，在网络吞吐量上优于传统启发式方法和集中式强化学习基线，并在拓扑和流量变化下具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络中的切换（HO）控制参数传统上依赖基于规则的启发式方法配置，其中小区个体偏移（CIO）是关键参数。然而，在网络规模上调整CIO是一个紧密耦合的问题：微小的变化可能影响多个邻居间的移动流，且静态规则在非平稳流量和移动性下性能下降。现有方法难以应对这种复杂性和动态性。

Method: 将切换优化问题建模为网络双重图上的分散式部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP），每个智能体控制一个邻居对CIO并观测其局部双重图邻域的聚合KPI，实现可扩展的分散决策。在此基础上，提出TD3-D-MA算法，这是一种离散多智能体强化学习变体，采用共享参数的图神经网络（GNN）演员网络在双重图上操作，并使用区域级双评论家网络进行训练，以改善密集部署中的信用分配问题。

Result: 在配置了现实网络运营商参数的ns-3系统级仿真器中，跨越异构流量场景和网络拓扑进行评估。结果表明，TD3-D-MA相比标准切换启发式方法和集中式RL基线，显著提高了网络吞吐量，并在拓扑和流量变化下表现出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过将切换优化问题转化为双重图上的分散式多智能体决策问题，并设计TD3-D-MA算法，成功解决了大规模蜂窝网络中CIO参数优化的挑战。该方法不仅提升了网络性能，还具备良好的可扩展性和泛化能力，为动态网络环境下的切换控制提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: HandOver (HO) control in cellular networks is governed by a set of HO control parameters that are traditionally configured through rule-based heuristics. A key parameter for HO optimization is the Cell Individual Offset (CIO), defined for each pair of neighboring cells and used to bias HO triggering decisions. At network scale, tuning CIOs becomes a tightly coupled problem: small changes can redirect mobility flows across multiple neighbors, and static rules often degrade under non-stationary traffic and mobility. We exploit the pairwise structure of CIOs by formulating HO optimization as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) on the network's dual graph. In this representation, each agent controls a neighbor-pair CIO and observes Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) aggregated over its local dual-graph neighborhood, enabling scalable decentralized decisions while preserving graph locality. Building on this formulation, we propose TD3-D-MA, a discrete Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) variant of the TD3 algorithm with a shared-parameter Graph Neural Network (GNN) actor operating on the dual graph and region-wise double critics for training, improving credit assignment in dense deployments. We evaluate TD3-D-MA in an ns-3 system-level simulator configured with real-world network operator parameters across heterogeneous traffic regimes and network topologies. Results show that TD3-D-MA improves network throughput over standard HO heuristics and centralized RL baselines, and generalizes robustly under topology and traffic shifts.

</details>


### [126] [Spatio-Temporal Semantic Inference for Resilient 6G HRLLC in the Low-Altitude Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24712)
*Chuan-Chi Lai,Ang-Hsun Tsai,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出EPIC框架，通过时空语义推断实现主动式协调，将端到端反应延迟降低93.5%，并在网络抖动和信号静默下保持确定性性能，为6G HRLLC提供关键支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济快速发展要求空中智能体间具备高可靠协同能力，然而6G网络中传统的反应式通信范式在城市峡谷等复杂环境下，易受随机网络抖动和间歇性信号静默影响，导致协同连接性不足。

Method: 引入具身主动推断协调（EPIC）框架，核心为时空语义推断（STSI）算子；该算子将过时的对等观测数据投影到主动信念流形上，使协调回路与物理信号波动解耦，从而在任意网络状态下维持确定性的反应延迟。

Result: 仿真实验表明，EPIC将端到端反应延迟平均降低93.5%，用确定性的10 ms执行心跳掩盖150 ms的物理传输延迟；在高达100 ms的网络抖动下表现出战略性免疫，并在长达50 s的极端信号静默期间将加权覆盖效率（WCE）提升10.5%。

Conclusion: EPIC框架证明了其在高度不确定网络环境下提供确定性韧性的能力，是实现6G超可靠低延迟通信（HRLLC）的关键使能技术。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Low-Altitude Economy (LAE) necessitates highly reliable coordination among autonomous aerial agents (AAAs). Traditional reactive communication paradigms in 6G networks are increasingly susceptible to stochastic network jitter and intermittent signaling silence, especially within complex urban canyon environments. To address this connectivity gap, this paper introduces the Embodied Proactive Inference for Coordination (EPIC) framework, featuring a Spatio-Temporal Semantic Inference (STSI) operator designed to decouple the coordination loop from physical signaling fluctuations. By projecting stale peer observations into a proactive belief manifold, EPIC maintains a deterministic reaction latency regardless of the network state. Extensive simulations demonstrate that EPIC achieves an average 93.5% reduction in end-to-end reaction latency, masking physical transmission delays of 150 ms with a deterministic 10 ms execution heartbeat. Crucially, EPIC exhibits strategic immunity to escalating network jitter up to 100 ms and improves the Weighted Coverage Efficiency (WCE) by 10.5% during extreme signaling silence lasting up to 50 s. These results provide the deterministic resilience essential for 6G Hyper-Reliable and Low-Latency Communication (HRLLC).

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Inspired Vehicular Network Optimization for Intelligent Decision Making in Smart Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24971)
*Kamran Ahmad Awan,Sonia Khan,Eman Abdullah Aldakheel,Saif Al-Kuwari,Ahmed Farouk*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了QIVNOM框架，一种在经典边云硬件上运行的量子启发式方法，通过联合优化V2V/V2I通信与城市交通控制，在仿真实验中实现了端到端延迟约20%的降低及可靠性与通行效率的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联网自动驾驶车辆需要在严格的延迟和可靠性约束下进行城市级协调，但现有方法通常将通信与移动性分开优化，这会导致在网络中断和计算资源竞争情况下性能下降。

Method: 提出了QIVNOM量子启发框架，在经典边云硬件上无需量子处理器即可运行。该框架将候选路由-信号方案编码为概率叠加态，使用球面投影梯度和退火采样进行更新；通过纠缠风格正则化器耦合网络与移动决策，并采用Tchebycheff多目标标量化配合可行性投影来强制执行延迟和可靠性约束。

Result: 在METR-LA校准的SUMO-OMNeT++/Veins仿真中，QIVNOM将平均端到端延迟降至57.3ms（比最佳基线低约20%）；在事故条件下延迟从79ms降至62ms（-21.5%），在RSU中断条件下从86ms降至67ms（-22.1%）；数据包交付率达96.7%（提升2.3个百分点），整体可靠性为96.7%；在走廊封闭场景中，平均行程时间降至12.8分钟，拥堵率降至33%。

Conclusion: QIVNOM框架证明了量子启发算法在经典硬件上联合优化通信与交通控制的可行性，能够在各种挑战性场景（如RSU中断、事故、走廊封闭）下显著降低延迟、提高可靠性并改善交通性能，为城市级联网自动驾驶车辆协调提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Connected and automated vehicles require city-scale coordination under strict latency and reliability constraints. However, many existing approaches optimize communication and mobility separately, which can degrade performance during network outages and under compute contention. This paper presents QIVNOM, a quantum-inspired framework that jointly optimizes vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) and vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) communication together with urban traffic control on classical edge--cloud hardware, without requiring a quantum processor. QIVNOM encodes candidate routing--signal plans as probabilistic superpositions and updates them using sphere-projected gradients with annealed sampling to minimize a regularized objective. An entanglement-style regularizer couples networking and mobility decisions, while Tchebycheff multi-objective scalarization with feasibility projection enforces constraints on latency and reliability.
  The proposed framework is evaluated in METR-LA--calibrated SUMO--OMNeT++/Veins simulations over a $5\times5$~km urban map with IEEE 802.11p and 5G NR sidelink. Results show that QIVNOM reduces mean end-to-end latency to 57.3~ms, approximately $20\%$ lower than the best baseline. Under incident conditions, latency decreases from 79~ms to 62~ms ($-21.5\%$), while under roadside unit (RSU) outages, it decreases from 86~ms to 67~ms ($-22.1\%$). Packet delivery reaches $96.7\%$ (an improvement of $+2.3$ percentage points), and reliability remains $96.7\%$ overall, including $96.8\%$ under RSU outages versus $94.1\%$ for the baseline. In corridor-closure scenarios, travel performance also improves, with average travel time reduced to 12.8~min and congestion lowered to $33\%$, compared with 14.5~min and $37\%$ for the baseline.

</details>


### [128] [A Wireless World Model for AI-Native 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25216)
*Ziqi Chen,Yi Ren,Yixuan Huang,Qi Sun,Nan Li,Yuhong Huang,Chih-Lin I,Yifan Li,Liang Xia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了无线世界模型（WWM），这是一个多模态基础框架，通过内化3D几何与信号动态之间的因果关系来预测无线信道的时空演化，在多个下游任务中均显著优于现有的单模态基础模型和任务特定模型，为物理感知的6G智能奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AI集成到物理层是6G网络的关键，但当前数据驱动方法缺乏对电磁波传播的内在理解，难以在动态环境中有效泛化。现有方法未能建立物理环境与信号传播之间的因果联系，导致在未见环境和实际场景中性能下降。

Method: 提出无线世界模型（WWM），采用联合嵌入预测架构和多模态混合专家Transformer。该方法在大规模射线追踪多模态数据集上进行预训练，融合信道状态信息、3D点云和用户轨迹为统一表示，内化3D几何与信号动态间的因果关系以预测无线信道的时空演化。

Result: WWM在五项关键下游任务中均取得显著性能，包括已见环境、未见泛化场景和真实世界测量数据。模型克服了数据真实性差距，在所有测试场景中始终优于最先进的单模态基础模型和任务特定模型，并通过真实世界测量数据得到验证。

Conclusion: WWM成功实现了物理感知的6G智能，能够适应物理世界的变化。该框架弥合了数据驱动方法与物理理解之间的鸿沟，为未来6G网络中AI与物理层的深度融合开辟了新道路。

Abstract: Integrating AI into the physical layer is a cornerstone of 6G networks. However, current data-driven approaches struggle to generalize across dynamic environments because they lack an intrinsic understanding of electromagnetic wave propagation. We introduce the Wireless World Model (WWM), a multi-modal foundation framework predicting the spatiotemporal evolution of wireless channels by internalizing the causal relationship between 3D geometry and signal dynamics. Pre-trained on a massive ray-traced multi-modal dataset, WWM overcomes the data authenticity gap, further validated under real-world measurement data. Using a joint-embedding predictive architecture with a multi-modal mixture-of-experts Transformer, WWM fuses channel state information, 3D point clouds, and user trajectories into a unified representation. Across the five key downstream tasks supported by WWM, it achieves remarkable performance in seen environments, unseen generalization scenarios, and real-world measurements, consistently outperforming SOTA uni-modal foundation models and task-specific models. This paves the way for physics-aware 6G intelligence that adapts to the physical world.

</details>


### [129] [Lightweight GenAI for Network Traffic Synthesis: Fidelity, Augmentation, and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25507)
*Giampaolo Bovenzi,Domenico Ciuonzo,Jonatan Krolikowski,Antonio Montieri,Alfredo Nascita,Antonio Pescapè,Dario Rossi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用轻量级生成式AI（GenAI）架构（包括Transformer、状态空间模型和扩散模型）解决网络流量生成（NTG）任务，在保证生成质量的同时降低了计算成本，有效缓解了流量分类任务中的数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的网络流量分类（NTC）日益受到标注数据有限和严格隐私要求的制约。虽然网络流量生成（NTG）是缓解数据稀缺的有效手段，但传统生成方法难以建模现代流量的复杂时序动态特性，且通常伴随着高昂的计算成本。

Method: 作者采用轻量级生成式人工智能架构进行流量生成，具体涵盖了基于Transformer的模型、状态空间模型和扩散模型。研究从四个维度进行了系统性评估：(i) 合成流量保真度，(ii) 纯合成数据训练，(iii) 低数据机制下的数据增强，以及 (iv) 计算效率。

Result: 在两个异构数据集上的实验表明，轻量级GenAI模型能很好地保持流量的静态和时序特征。其中，Transformer和状态空间模型在所有保真度指标上均能紧密匹配真实分布。仅使用合成流量训练的分类器在真实数据上达到了高达87%的F1分数。在低数据设置下，GenAI驱动的数据增强将NTC性能提升了高达40%，显著缩小了与全数据训练的差距。

Conclusion: 总体而言，基于Transformer的模型在保真度和效率之间提供了最佳平衡，能够以适度的计算开销实现高质量、隐私感知的流量合成，为解决数据稀缺和隐私保护问题提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Accurate Network Traffic Classification (NTC) is increasingly constrained by limited labeled data and strict privacy requirements. While Network Traffic Generation (NTG) provides an effective means to mitigate data scarcity, conventional generative methods struggle to model the complex temporal dynamics of modern traffic or/and often incur significant computational cost. In this article, we address the NTG task using lightweight Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) architectures, including transformer-based, state-space, and diffusion models designed for practical deployment. We conduct a systematic evaluation along four axes: (i) (synthetic) traffic fidelity, (ii) synthetic-only training, (iii) data augmentation under low-data regimes, and (iv) computational efficiency. Experiments on two heterogeneous datasets show that lightweight GenAI models preserve both static and temporal traffic characteristics, with transformer and state-space models closely matching real distributions across a complete set of fidelity metrics. Classifiers trained solely on synthetic traffic achieve up to 87% F1-score on real data. In low-data settings, GenAI-driven augmentation improves NTC performance by up to +40%, substantially reducing the gap with full-data training. Overall, transformer-based models provide the best trade-off between fidelity and efficiency, enabling high-quality, privacy-aware traffic synthesis with modest computational overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Belief-Driven Multi-Agent Collaboration via Approximate Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium for Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24973)
*Weiwei Fang,Lin Li,Kaize Shi,Yu Yang,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了BEACOF框架，一种基于完美贝叶斯均衡的信念驱动自适应协作方法，解决了现有多智能体框架静态交互拓扑导致的模拟失真问题，在司法、社交和医疗场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体框架主要采用静态交互拓扑，无法捕捉真实世界中合作知识综合与竞争批判推理之间的动态转换，导致"群体思维"或无效僵局，影响了社会模拟的可信度。

Method: 受完美贝叶斯均衡(PBE)启发，将社会交互建模为不完全信息动态博弈，通过迭代更新对同伴能力的概率信念，自主调节协作策略，在不确定性下确保序贯理性决策，解决了协作类型选择与能力估计之间的循环依赖问题。

Result: 在对抗性(司法)、开放式(社交)和混合(医疗)三类场景中验证，BEACOF有效避免了协调失败，促进了向高质量解的稳健收敛，展现了可靠社会模拟的优越潜力。

Conclusion: BEACOF框架通过动态博弈建模和信念驱动的自适应协作机制，成功实现了更真实、可靠的社会模拟，为复杂Web社会挑战提供了有效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: High-fidelity social simulation is pivotal for addressing complex Web societal challenges, yet it demands agents capable of authentically replicating the dynamic spectrum of human interaction. Current LLM-based multi-agent frameworks, however, predominantly adhere to static interaction topologies, failing to capture the fluid oscillation between cooperative knowledge synthesis and competitive critical reasoning seen in real-world scenarios. This rigidity often leads to unrealistic ``groupthink'' or unproductive deadlocks, undermining the credibility of simulations for decision support. To bridge this gap, we propose \textit{BEACOF}, a \textit{belief-driven adaptive collaboration framework} inspired by Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium (PBE). By modeling social interaction as a dynamic game of incomplete information, BEACOF rigorously addresses the circular dependency between collaboration type selection and capability estimation. Agents iteratively refine probabilistic beliefs about peer capabilities and autonomously modulate their collaboration strategy, thereby ensuring sequentially rational decisions under uncertainty. Validated across adversarial (judicial), open-ended (social) and mixed (medical) scenarios, BEACOF prevents coordination failures and fosters robust convergence toward high-quality solutions, demonstrating superior potential for reliable social simulation. Source codes and datasets are publicly released at: https://github.com/WUT-IDEA/BEACOF.

</details>


### [131] [Ultra-fast Traffic Nowcasting and Control via Differentiable Agent-based Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25068)
*Fumiyasu Makinoshima,Yuya Yamaguchi,Eigo Segawa,Koichiro Niinuma,Sean Qian*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微分智能体交通仿真器，在大规模路网上实现了超快速的模型校准、交通临近预报与控制，为交通数字孪生提供了可行的计算基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通数字孪生有望为城市交通决策提供支持，但传统细粒度交通仿真不可微分，依赖低效的无梯度优化方法，导致大规模场景下的模型校准在计算上不可行。

Method: 开发了多种可微分计算技术来模拟单个车辆运动，包括随机决策和智能体间交互，同时确保整个仿真轨迹端到端可微分，从而支持高效的基于梯度的优化。

Result: 在拥有超过10,000个校准参数的大规模芝加哥路网中，该模型以173倍实时速度模拟超过100万辆车。利用前30分钟的交通数据，在455秒内完成模型校准，在21秒内提供未来一小时的交通临近预报，并在728秒内求解相应的交通控制问题。

Conclusion: 该方法在20分钟内完成完整的校准—临近预报—控制循环，留出约40分钟的实施干预准备时间，为实现交通数字孪生提供了实用的计算基础。

Abstract: Traffic digital twins, which inform policymakers of effective interventions based on large-scale, high-fidelity computational models calibrated to real-world traffic, hold promise for addressing societal challenges in our rapidly urbanizing world. However, conventional fine-grained traffic simulations are non-differentiable and typically rely on inefficient gradient-free optimization, making calibration for real-world applications computationally infeasible. Here we present a differentiable agent-based traffic simulator that enables ultra-fast model calibration, traffic nowcasting, and control on large-scale networks. We develop several differentiable computing techniques for simulating individual vehicle movements, including stochastic decision-making and inter-agent interactions, while ensuring that entire simulation trajectories remain end-to-end differentiable for efficient gradient-based optimization. On the large-scale Chicago road network, with over 10,000 calibration parameters, our model simulates more than one million vehicles at 173 times real-time speed. This ultra-fast simulation, together with efficient gradient-based optimization, enables us to complete model calibration using the previous 30 minutes of traffic data in 455 s, provide a one-hour-ahead traffic nowcast in 21 s, and solve the resulting traffic control problem in 728 s. This yields a full calibration--nowcast--control loop in under 20 minutes, leaving about 40 minutes of lead time for implementing interventions. Our work thus provides a practical computational basis for realizing traffic digital twins.

</details>


### [132] [From Logic Monopoly to Social Contract: Separation of Power and the Institutional Foundations for Autonomous Agent Economies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25100)
*Anbang Ruan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出AE4E范式，通过建立“权力分立”的社会契约架构（立法、执行、司法三权分立），解决多智能体系统中的“逻辑垄断”与可靠性差距问题，并依托NetX企业框架实现从私有飞地到全球服务网络的规模化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架存在结构缺陷，即每个智能体同时拥有规划、执行和评估自身行动的权力，形成“逻辑垄断”。这导致了严重的可靠性差距：十个部署场景中平均攻击成功率达84.30%，在没有明确奖励信号的情况下有31.4%出现欺骗行为，且存在六个结构性瓶颈导致的级联故障模式。

Method: 引入企业版智能体（AE4E）范式，将智能体视为功能主义社会系统中自主且具有法律身份的商业实体；构建以契约为中心的权力分立（SoP）模型，将权威三分化为立法、执行和司法部门。通过NetX企业框架（NEF）实现该范式：治理枢纽、TEE背书的计算飞地、隐私保护数据桥和原生智能体区块链底层。基于帕森斯AGIL框架构建智能体社会层，提供六十多个机构性AE4E。

Result: 建立了涵盖四个部署层级（从私有飞地到全球服务网络）的智能体企业经济体系，设计了八个专用智能合约，并提出了可操作的制度基础设施方案以系统性解决可靠性差距问题。

Conclusion: 解决智能体可靠性问题的核心不在于单个模型的对齐，而在于为智能体建立社会契约：即通过制度基础设施强制执行宪法级的权力分立，从而从根本上消除逻辑垄断带来的结构性风险。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent frameworks allow each agent to simultaneously plan, execute, and evaluate its own actions -- a structural deficiency we term the "Logic Monopoly." Empirical evidence quantifies the resulting "Reliability Gap": 84.30% average attack success rates across ten deployment scenarios, 31.4% emergent deceptive behavior without explicit reward signals, and cascading failure modes rooted in six structural bottlenecks.
  The remedy is not better alignment of individual models but a social contract for agents: institutional infrastructure that enforces a constitutional Separation of Power. This paper introduces the Agent Enterprise for Enterprise (AE4E) paradigm -- agents as autonomous, legally identifiable business entities within a functionalist social system -- with a contract-centric SoP model trifurcating authority into Legislation, Execution, and Adjudication branches. The paradigm is operationalized through the NetX Enterprise Framework (NEF): governance hubs, TEE-backed compute enclaves, privacy-preserving data bridges, and an Agent-Native blockchain substrate. The Agent Enterprise Economy scales across four deployment tiers from private enclaves to a global Web of Services. The Agentic Social Layer, grounded in Parsons' AGIL framework, provides institutional infrastructure via sixty-plus named Institutional AE4Es. 143 pages, 173 references, eight specialized smart contracts.

</details>


### [133] [AD-CARE: A Guideline-grounded, Modality-agnostic LLM Agent for Real-world Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis with Multi-cohort Assessment, Fairness Analysis, and Reader Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25322)
*Wenlong Hou,Sheng Bi,Guangqian Yang,Lihao Liu,Ye Du,Hanxiao Xue,Juncheng Wang,Yuxiang Feng,Yue Xun,Nanxi Yu,Ning Mao,Mo Yang,Yi Wah Eva Cheung,Ling Long,Kay Chen Tan,Lequan Yu,Xiaomeng Ma,Shaozhen Yan,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了AD-CARE，一个模态无关的LLM智能体，能够基于不完整、异构的多模态数据生成透明的阿尔茨海默病诊断报告，在六千余例病例中诊断准确率达84.9%，显著减少了种族和年龄亚组间的性能差异，并有效提升了临床医生的诊断准确率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）是全球老龄化面临的重大健康挑战，及时准确的诊断至关重要。然而，现实世界中的AD评估面临数据不完整、多模态异构性以及跨中心和患者人口统计学差异等问题。现有的医学大语言模型（LLM）应用多局限于回答特定的疾病问题，缺乏能够支持临床决策的综合诊断报告生成能力。

Method: 研究团队引入了AD-CARE框架，这是一个模态无关的智能体。其核心方法包括：1) 不对缺失模态进行填补，直接处理不完整、异构的输入数据；2) 动态编排专业的诊断工具；3) 将临床指南嵌入LLM驱动的推理过程中，从而生成符合现实临床工作流程的透明、报告式诊断输出。

Result: 在包含10,303个病例的六个队列中，AD-CARE实现了84.9%的诊断准确率，相较于基线方法有4.2%-13.7%的相对提升。各数据集准确率保持在80.4%-98.8%的稳健水平。该框架显著减少了种族和年龄亚组间的性能差异，平均离散度分别降低了21%-68%和28%-51%。在读者研究中，它将神经科医生和放射科医生的准确率提升了6%-11%，决策时间缩短了一半以上。此外，该框架在八种骨干LLM上均取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: AD-CARE是一个可扩展且具实际部署价值的框架，证明了通过将临床指南嵌入推理过程并动态编排工具，LLM可以有效地处理现实世界中不完整的多模态数据。该框架能够无缝集成到常规临床工作流程中，为阿尔茨海默病的多模态决策支持提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a growing global health challenge as populations age, and timely, accurate diagnosis is essential to reduce individual and societal burden. However, real-world AD assessment is hampered by incomplete, heterogeneous multimodal data and variability across sites and patient demographics. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in biomedicine, their use in AD has largely been confined to answering narrow, disease-specific questions rather than generating comprehensive diagnostic reports that support clinical decision-making. Here we expand LLM capabilities for clinical decision support by introducing AD-CARE, a modality-agnostic agent that performs guideline-grounded diagnostic assessment from incomplete, heterogeneous inputs without imputing missing modalities. By dynamically orchestrating specialized diagnostic tools and embedding clinical guidelines into LLM-driven reasoning, AD-CARE generates transparent, report-style outputs aligned with real-world clinical workflows. Across six cohorts comprising 10,303 cases, AD-CARE achieved 84.9% diagnostic accuracy, delivering 4.2%-13.7% relative improvements over baseline methods. Despite cohort-level differences, dataset-specific accuracies remain robust (80.4%-98.8%), and the agent consistently outperforms all baselines. AD-CARE reduced performance disparities across racial and age subgroups, decreasing the average dispersion of four metrics by 21%-68% and 28%-51%, respectively. In a controlled reader study, the agent improved neurologist and radiologist accuracy by 6%-11% and more than halved decision time. The framework yielded 2.29%-10.66% absolute gains over eight backbone LLMs and converges their performance. These results show that AD-CARE is a scalable, practically deployable framework that can be integrated into routine clinical workflows for multimodal decision support in AD.

</details>


### [134] [Conchordal: Emergent Harmony via Direct Cognitive Coupling in a Psychoacoustic Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25637)
*Koichi Takahashi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Conchordal，一种基于人工生命动力学的生物声学生成式作曲系统，通过心理声学适应度地形实现自组织多声部音乐的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种不依赖预设离散音阶或显式和声规则的生成式作曲方法，通过直接认知耦合（DCC）设计原则，使生成动力学直接在心理声学可观测变量导出的地形中运行，从而实现更自然的音乐自组织。

Method: 构建了一个心理声学导出的连续协和度地形（整合粗糙度与和谐性），其中的声音智能体通过局部提议-接受动态调整音高（带拥挤惩罚），通过协和度依赖的代谢调节生存，并通过Kuramoto式相位耦合实现时间同步。

Result: 四个实验表明：（1）协和度搜索产生结构化多声部，富含协和音程；（2）协和度依赖代谢产生生存差异，禁用补给时差异消失；（3）最小遗传适应实验显示亲代引导重生加代谢选择可在无成年期爬坡的情况下积累结构化多声部；（4）共享振荡支架在外部驱动下组织节奏时序。

Conclusion: 心理声学导出的地形可作为有效的人工生命地形，在非传统计算媒介中实现自组织、选择、同步和谱系级积累，同一地形既作为生态地形又作为音乐连贯性的内部代理。

Abstract: This paper introduces Conchordal, a bio-acoustic instrument for generative composition whose sonic agents are governed by artificial life dynamics within a psychoacoustic fitness landscape. The system is built on Direct Cognitive Coupling (DCC), a design principle requiring that generative dynamics operate directly within a landscape derived from psychoacoustic observables and read from that landscape without symbolic harmonic rules. The environment integrates roughness and harmonicity into a continuous consonance field without presupposing discrete scales or explicit harmonic rules. Agents adjust pitch through local proposal-and-accept dynamics under a crowding penalty, regulate survival via consonance-dependent metabolism, and entrain temporally through Kuramoto-style phase coupling. Four experiments are reported: (1) consonance search produces structured polyphony with enriched consonant intervals; (2) consonance-dependent metabolism yields survival differentials that vanish when recharge is disabled; (3) a minimal hereditary adaptation assay shows that parent-guided respawn plus metabolic selection can accumulate more structured polyphony without adult hill-climbing; and (4) a shared oscillatory scaffold organizes rhythmic timing under external forcing. A supplementary mechanism check reports one possible composer-configurable bridge by which spectral state can modulate temporal coupling. These findings show that a psychoacoustically derived landscape serves as an effective artificial-life terrain, yielding self-organization, selection, synchronization, and lineage-level accumulation in a non-traditional computational medium. At the level of the model, the same landscape therefore functions both as ecological terrain and as an internal proxy for musical coherence.

</details>
